This paper develops an exact multistate reliability framework in which each service unit carries an arbitrary finite set of bandwidth states and reliability is the probability that aggregate delivered bandwidth meets a demand. The binary k-out-of-n model is recovered as a special case, while closed-form binary-mapping error relations quantify mean-bandwidth distortion and provide a screening test for the simpler abstraction. For exact single-threshold evaluation a threshold-pruned multistate Binary-Addition-Tree (TP-mBAT) algorithm is proposed. It is deliberately regime-specific: fixed-grid dynamic programming is preferable on a compact common grid, where a 16-unit commensurate control required 273 pruned-DP updates versus 362,506 TP-mBAT node visits. On a reproducible 14-unit incommensurate benchmark, TP-mBAT is compared against a dynamic program carrying the same threshold rules, so that no baseline is weakened. Both expand the same state space, 6,862 nodes against 6,861 updates, and the separation lies entirely in retained state: 17 traversal entries against 412,121 probability states at central demand, reducing measured peak storage from 25.02 MB to 1,152 B. An exact probability-transfer sensitivity identifies when moving mass from a degraded state to a higher-bandwidth state changes system success, and a reserved-unit floor model admits a third exact pruning rule that is vacuous without such floors. A latent package-state mixture captures shared stress, where ignoring dependence overstates reliability by 8.73 percentage points.

</details>


### [71] [A Thread-Register Decoupled GPU Execution Model for Efficient Tensor Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19628)
*Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yangjie Zhou,Yitong Ding,Guanlin Zhu,Yilu Huang,Chiheng Jin,Chen Zhang,Shixuan Sun,Yu Feng,Anbang Wu,Minyi Guo,Jian Weng,Jiajin Tu,Junsong Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FIBER提出了一种扩展GPU SIMT模型的新型架构，通过解耦执行实例与私有寄存器所有权，实现动态并行度扩展和细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，在LLM服务场景下实现最高2.25倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU虽集成Tensor Cores且张量吞吐量持续增长，但固定并行度和粗粒度调度导致现代AI工作负载（GEMM与非GEMM操作交错）的张量计算流水线编排效率低下，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出FIBER架构扩展GPU SIMT模型：将基本执行单元fiber与私有寄存器所有权解耦，仅携带最小控制状态，通过共享视图访问SM寄存器；扩展ISA、微架构和编译器实现共享寄存器寻址、无冲突操作数交付及fiber级程序映射。

Result: 在混合精度LLM服务场景下，Ampere架构实现2.25倍端到端加速（原始FP16计算1.15倍），Hopper和Blackwell分别达到1.8倍和2.09倍，内核级加速最高2.49倍。

Conclusion: FIBER通过动态并行度扩展和细粒度寄存器级数据流调度，有效解决了现代AI工作负载中张量计算流水线编排的低效问题，为矩阵操作数供应提供了无冗余替代方案，显著提升了多代GPU架构的性能。

Abstract: Modern GPUs increasingly integrate Tensor Cores into the execution pipeline. Although aggregate tensor throughput continues to grow, aided by an operand supply that has evolved from register-based in Ampere to redundancy-free, memory-based in Hopper and Blackwell, efficiently orchestrating the complete tensor compute pipeline for the modern AI workloads remains challenging. We identify the fundamental bottlenecks as fixed parallelism and coarse-grained scheduling, both of which are exposed by modern AI workloads that interleave diverse non-GEMM operations with GEMM. To orchestrate tensor computation efficiently, we propose FIBER, a new architecture that extends the GPU SIMT (single instruction, multiple thread) model. Its basic execution instance, the \emph{fiber}, is decoupled from private register ownership, carrying only minimal control state while accessing an SM's registers through a shared view. This enables dynamic parallelism scaling, fine-grained register-level dataflow scheduling, and offers a redundancy-free alternative for matrix operand supply. We extend the ISA, microarchitecture, and compiler to realize shared-register addressing, conflict-free operand delivery, and fiber-based program mapping. Under a typical mixed-precision LLM serving scenario, FIBER achieves a 2.25x end-to-end speedup on Ampere (1.15x for the original FP16 computation), with 1.8x and 2.09x on Hopper and Blackwell respectively, and kernel-level gains up to 2.49x.

</details>


### [72] [Automated Estimation of MBIST Area and Test Time in Heterogeneous Memory IPs via Stacked Ensemble Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19705)
*Chee Jin Teoh,Ab Al-Hadi Ab Rahman,Johnny Kee Hui Wong,Premkumar A/L Kesavan Prabagaran,Muhammad Nadzir Marsono,Nuzhat Khan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于监督学习的框架，可在无需综合的情况下直接从RTL设计参数预测MBIST的面积和测试时间开销，面积预测准确率达90.68%，测试时间预测准确率达96.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式存储器在SoC设计中占据较大比例，MBIST虽能确保存储器可靠性但会引入额外的面积和测试时间开销。传统的开销估算方法依赖完整的RTL综合和测试模式生成，速度慢且资源消耗大，无法满足设计规划阶段快速准确估算的需求。

Method: 使用Synopsys Design Compiler和Intel增强的MINT工具生成数据集（面积样本4470个，测试时间样本624个），输入特征包括存储器数量、字宽、地址深度、端口配置和时钟域。面积预测采用多项式扩展、对数变换和缩放处理特征，通过XGBoost、LightGBM和神经网络的堆叠集成模型，以梯度提升作为元学习器；测试时间预测使用XGBoost和LightGBM结合岭回归，通过100次Optuna搜索优化超参数。

Result: 面积预测准确率达到90.68%，测试时间预测准确率达到96.80%（±10%误差范围内），相比基线方法分别提升8.53%和48.80%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在设计早期阶段快速估算MBIST开销，避免了耗时的综合过程，支持存储器IP开发中更高效的设计决策，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Embedded memories occupy a large portion of modern System-on-Chip (SoC) designs, especially in high-performance applications such as artificial intelligence and edge computing. Memory Built-In Self-Test (MBIST) is commonly used to ensure memory reliability, but it introduces additional area and test time overhead. Accurate early estimation of these overheads is important during design planning, yet conventional methods rely on full Register Transfer Level (RTL) synthesis and test pattern generation, which are slow and resource-intensive. This study proposes a supervised learning framework that predicts MBIST area and test time directly from RTL-level design parameters without synthesis. A dataset of 4,470 samples for area and 624 for test time was generated using Synopsys Design Compiler and MINT, an Intel-enhanced MBIST tool. Input features include memory count, word width, address depth, port configuration, and clock domains. For area prediction, the features are processed through polynomial expansion, log transformation, and scaling, followed by a stacked ensemble model using XGBoost, LightGBM, and a Neural Network with Gradient Boosting as the meta-learner. For test time, XGBoost and LightGBM are combined using Ridge Regression, with hyperparameters tuned through a 100-trial Optuna search. The models achieved 90.68% accuracy for area and 96.80% for test time within a +/-10% margin, improving over baseline methods by 8.53% and 48.80% respectively. The results show that this approach enables faster estimation of MBIST costs and supports more efficient design decisions in memory IP development.

</details>


### [73] [Energy-Efficient Visual Inspection with FFT-Based CNNs and Adaptive Floating-Point Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19837)
*Lukas Krupp,Marco Groß,Michael Graichen,Kim Ulrich,Norbert Wehn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究在工业CPU-FPGA平台上利用FP8量化进行FFT-based CNN推理，通过层级偏置优化将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%，相比CPU-only推理能效提升约2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的FPGA平台上部署CNN推理时，FFT-based卷积计算需要应对低精度浮点运算带来的精度损失问题，同时需要在保持硬件效率的前提下提升模型准确性。

Method: 结合FFT-based卷积与自适应训练后FP8量化，评估两种FPGA优化方法：FFT内部的渐进式偏置调整(PBA)和跨CNN层级的指数偏置选择，在LeNet-5加速器中使用串行radix-$2^2$ SDF FFT模块实现。

Result: 权重缩放性能优于PBA；层级偏置优化将准确率从80.33%提升至84.13%且无需修改数据通路宽度；相比CPU-only推理，FPGA实现约2.5倍更高的能效。

Conclusion: 层级偏置优化是在不增加硬件资源的前提下提升FFT-based CNN推理精度的有效方法，工业CPU-FPGA平台在低精度量化场景下相比纯CPU方案具有显著的能效优势。

Abstract: This paper investigates reduced-precision floating-point arithmetic for FFT-based CNN inference on an industrial CPU-FPGA platform. We combine FFT-based convolution with adaptive post-training FP8 quantization and evaluate two FPGA-oriented optimization methods: progressive bias adjustment (PBA) within the FFT and layer-wise exponent-bias selection across the CNN. The methods are implemented in a LeNet-5 accelerator using serial radix-$2^2$ SDF FFT modules and evaluated on an industrial fault detection dataset. Results show that weight scaling outperforms PBA, while layer-wise bias optimization increases the accuracy from 80.33% to 84.13% without modifying the datapath width. Compared with CPU-only inference, the FPGA achieves approximately 2.5$\times$ higher energy efficiency.

</details>


### [74] [Experimental Verification of Fast Voltage Droop Correction Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19954)
*Shreyas Srinivas,Ian W. Jones,Carsten Schulze,Milos Krstic,Christoph Lenzen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种用于电压跌落自适应响应的全数字快速电路，可在节省能耗的同时缓解VLSI电路的频率限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为节能目的而最小化保护频带的趋势导致电压跌落成为限制当今VLSI电路工作频率的关键因素，而动态调整时钟频率面临检测和存储电压跌落状态时的亚稳态挑战。

Method: 使用IHP 130nm工艺实现一种快速的全数字电路，用于对电压跌落进行自适应响应，并在测试芯片上实验验证设计的功能。

Result: 成功在测试芯片上实验验证了所提出的全数字自适应电压跌落响应电路的功能性。

Conclusion: 所提出的全数字电路能够有效应对VLSI电路中的电压跌落问题，为动态频率调整提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Due to the trend towards minimizing guard bands for energy saving purposes, voltage droops are a key limiting factor for the operational frequency of today's VLSI circuits. Adapting clock frequencies dynamically presents the challenge of metastability in the device that detects and stores the existence of voltage droops. We present an implementation of a fast all-digital circuit for adaptive response to droops using the IHP 130 nm process. The description of the design is presented in an accompanying paper. We experimentally validate the functionality of the design on a test chip.

</details>


### [75] [ODEONN: A Digital ODE Solver Architecture for Oscillatory Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20110)
*Bram F. Haverkort,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出首个支持复值耦合的全数字振荡神经网络通用架构ODEONN，通过定点量化和正弦函数近似实现45倍能效提升，性能损失小于2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字振荡神经网络架构通常针对特定应用设计，缺乏通用性，限制了ONN在AI和组合优化问题中的广泛应用。

Method: 提出模块化可扩展架构ODEONN，支持多种ONN应用；引入复值耦合支持；设计一种新型正弦函数近似方法，硬件资源消耗仅为传统方法的一半；采用定点量化实现。

Result: 与全精度软件仿真相比性能下降小于2%，验证了定点量化和近似波形对计算精度影响极小；能效延迟积相比传统硬件上的软件仿真降低45倍。

Conclusion: ODEONN成功实现了通用性强、硬件效率高、精度损失小的数字振荡神经网络架构，为ONN的实际部署提供了有效的数字硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Oscillatory Neural Networks (ONNs) are an alternative computing paradigm for AI and combinatorial optimization problems. However, digital architectures are often designed for specific applications of ONNs. This work introduces a modular and scalable architecture called ODEONN that is generic to multiple applications of ONNs, and to the best of our knowledge, is the first fully digital ONN to also support complex-valued coupling. Additionally, an approximation of the sine function is introduced that uses half of the hardware resources compared to standard methods. The performance of ODEONN is compared with a full-precision software simulation, where a performance degradation of less than $2\%$ is shown. Therefore, we conclude that the fixed-point quantization and the approximated waveform affect the accuracy of computation by only a small amount. Furthermore, ODEONN shows a 45$\times$ reduction in energy-delay product over the software simulation running on conventional hardware.

</details>


### [76] [A Resource-Efficient CNN-Based EEG Auditory Attention Decoding ASIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20198)
*Qier Ma,Richard George,Stefan Scholze,Jehn Constantin,Tobias Reichenbach,Christian Mayr*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于实时EEG听觉注意力解码的资源高效ASIC芯片，通过量化CNN推理引擎和皮尔逊相关分类器实现，在22nm工艺下功耗仅0.49mW，延迟7.34ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人工耳蜗（CI）用户在嘈杂环境中的"鸡尾酒会问题"（跟随目标说话者），现有研究已探索基于EEG的听觉注意力解码（AAD）神经网络方法，但需要高效的硬件平台实现实时处理。

Method: 集成量化CNN推理引擎和皮尔逊相关分类器；采用流式执行（streaming execution）、片上缓冲（on-chip buffering）和内存高效数据流（memory-efficient dataflow）架构以降低硬件开销并保持实时性能。

Result: 在GF22FDX 22nm CMOS工艺实现，总硅面积2.09 mm²（CNN推理引擎和流分类引擎仅占0.076 mm²）；核心电压0.55V下功耗0.4941 mW，推理延迟7.34 ms。

Conclusion: 该ASIC为助听应用中的EEG听觉注意力解码提供了能效高的硬件平台，实现了资源高效、实时的处理能力。

Abstract: Following a target speaker in a noisy environment, commonly known as the cocktail party problem, remains particularly challenging for cochlear implant (CI) users. Recent studies have explored EEG-based auditory attention decoding (AAD) using neural networks to enhance hearing assistance. This paper presents a resource-efficient ASIC for real-time EEG-based auditory attention decoding by integrating a quantized CNN inference engine and a Pearson-correlation classifier. The proposed architecture employs streaming execution, on-chip buffering, and memory-efficient dataflow to reduce hardware cost while maintaining real-time performance.
  The proposed ASIC has been fully implemented in GF22FDX 22-nm CMOS technology, occupying a total silicon area of 2.09 mm$^2$(1264$μ$m x 1654$μ$m), with the CNN inference engine and streaming classification engine requiring only 0.076 mm$^2$. Operating at a core voltage of 0.55 V, the design achieves a power consumption of 0.4941 mW and an inference latency of 7.34 ms, providing an energy-efficient hardware platform for EEG-based auditory attention decoding in hearing-assistance applications.

</details>

### [77] [The Lazy Pod That Lies: Deferred Cost and Failure Semantics of Lazy Container Image Pulling for Model Serving on Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19412)
*Georgii Kliukovkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了容器镜像懒加载（lazy pulling）在Kubernetes模型服务中的性能，发现虽然将冷启动时间从24.5-573秒缩短到17秒左右，但将成本推迟而非消除，并在持续读取时引入了新故障模式：节点级缓存耗尽会导致运行中Pod的模型文件读取失败（最高94%），且监控机制存在盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型服务Pod启动时镜像拉取是主要瓶颈，懒加载技术承诺通过立即挂载镜像并按需获取内容来消除此成本。作者希望验证这一承诺在真实生产环境中的表现，特别是针对大型AI模型（2-140GB）的服务场景。

Method: 在Kubernetes KServe平台上评估两种生产级懒加载系统（eStargz/stargz-snapshotter和AWS SOCI），使用真实fp16权重模型（2-140GB），对比急切拉取（eager pulling）基线，测量冷启动时间、读取性能、缓存行为和故障模式。

Result: 1）冷启动时间从24.5-573秒降至16.9-17.6秒，确实与镜像大小无关；2）成本被推迟：14GB模型完整读取需105.3秒，比急切拉取的72.4秒更慢；3）两个系统成本分布在生命周期两端（SOCI预取几乎全部镜像，eStargz延迟所有读取）；4）发现缓存耗尽故障模式：持续合法读取下，67-94%模型文件失败；5）监控盲区：Pod在196秒内通过所有K8s检查但实际已失败。

Conclusion: 懒加载技术将启动成本推迟而非消除，并引入急切拉取不存在的缓存耗尽故障风险。需要为服务平台和运维人员提供放置策略、监控机制和缓存容量配置指导，特别是在处理大型AI模型时需要重新评估懒加载的实际收益和风险。

Abstract: Lazy container-image pulling promises to eliminate the dominant cost of starting a model-serving pod by mounting the image immediately and fetching content on demand. We evaluate this promise for model delivery on Kubernetes, using KServe with two production lazy-pulling systems -- eStargz/stargz-snapshotter and AWS SOCI -- against eager baselines, on artifacts from 2 to 140 GB including real fp16 weights. Lazy pulling delivers its headline: cold time-to-first-prediction becomes size-independent (16.9--17.6s, versus 24.5--573.0s eager). But the cost is deferred, not eliminated: a full read of a 14 GB model through the lazy mount takes 105.3s, slower than the 72.4s eager pull it replaced, and the two systems pay at opposite lifecycle ends (SOCI prefetches nearly the full image before Ready; eStargz defers nearly everything to first read). More consequentially, we characterize a failure mode eager pulling structurally cannot exhibit: under sustained legitimate reads with default configuration, the snapshotter's node-level cache exhausts its finite volume and already-running pods begin failing reads of model files. At the earliest stage of exhaustion, an instrumented serving pod passed every Kubernetes-visible and application-level check for 196s while its snapshotter was already logging real failures; under heavier pressure, 67--94% of model files fail, scaling monotonically with residual cache occupancy. A live pod self-heals if cache space is freed under it, but a snapshotter-daemon restart under a live pod leaves permanently stale file handles in a pod still reported Running. We derive placement, monitoring, and cache-sizing guidance for serving platforms and operators.

</details>


### [78] [When Do LLM Agents Help? Deadline-Aware Mixed-Criticality Task Scheduling at the Autonomous-Vehicle Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19557)
*Reza Zakerian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了异构MEC服务器上的截止期感知混合关键性调度。研究分两步：首先提出了一种窗口化合同网拍卖启发式算法，在TC任务完成率上达到0.902，超越所有15个基线；其次引入多智能体LLM控制层，发现LLM仅在非平稳负载（如关键任务激增）时优于静态启发式和Bandit算法，在平稳负载下启发式已接近最优，LLM无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆将延迟敏感的感知任务卸载到附近MEC服务器，错过安全关键任务是不安全的。LLMs被提议作为自适应、可解释的调度器，但缺乏关于其何时有用的证据。研究旨在回答多智能体LLM控制层是否能改进强启发式算法。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) 构建窗口化合同网拍卖启发式算法，按最早截止期对每个接纳窗口进行时间关键优先排序，并按最早完成时间放置任务；2) 添加LLM控制平面，通过控制性分解分析调度器优势来源，比较平稳负载和非平稳负载（安全关键任务激增）下的性能。

Result: 启发式算法在60个实例、三种拓扑、15个基线中TC完成率达0.902，优于所有基线（Holm校正p<0.001，最佳基线0.838），达到CP-SAT上界的0.87。LLM控制层在平稳负载下无增益，但在安全关键任务激增时显著优于静态启发式和Bandit。LLM编排仅在非平稳性打开固定策略无法利用的余地时才值得其成本。

Conclusion: LLM控制层的价值取决于负载特性：在平稳负载下，精心设计的启发式算法已接近最优，LLM带来的开销无收益；但在非平稳场景（如任务激增）下，LLM能利用固定策略无法使用的余地，显著提升性能。这为LLM在边缘计算调度中的应用提供了明确的适用条件指导。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offload latency-sensitive perception tasks to nearby mobile edge computing (MEC) servers, where a missed safety-critical task is unsafe rather than merely degraded. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as adaptive, explainable schedulers, yet evidence of when they help is scarce. We study deadline-aware, mixed-criticality scheduling on heterogeneous MEC servers, where time-critical (TC) tasks must be protected at a controlled cost to best-effort traffic, and ask whether a multi-agent LLM control layer improves on a strong heuristic. We answer in two steps. First we build the heuristic: a windowed contract-net auction that orders each admission window time-critical-first by earliest deadline and places tasks by earliest-finish-time. Across 60 instances on three topologies and 15 baselines under an identical online constraint, it attains a TC completion rate of 0.902, above every baseline (Holm-corrected p < 0.001; best baseline 0.838) and at 0.87 of a CP-SAT upper bound. Second, we add the LLM control plane. A controlled decomposition traces the scheduler's advantage to two ordinary factors, the batching horizon and time-critical-first ordering; the auction, the per-window LLM policy, and online adaptation add nothing while the load is stationary, where the heuristic is already near-optimal. Under a mid-run surge of safety-critical tasks the picture changes, and the LLM control plane gains significantly over both the static heuristic and the bandit. LLM orchestration therefore earns its cost only when non-stationarity opens headroom a fixed policy cannot use. We report control-plane latency and rationale, and release all code and seeded instances.

</details>


### [79] [Optimality and Trade-offs in Fast BFT SMR (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19629)
*Neil Giridharan,Minh Tung Nguyen,Ittai Abraham,Allen Clement,Natacha Crooks,Pierre Sutra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对拜占庭容错环境下的快速状态机复制协议，提出了复制因子的紧确界，并给出了一个在复制因子和恢复效率之间进行权衡的次优协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 崩溃故障模型下的快速状态机复制协议已在学术界和工业界得到广泛关注，具有低延迟、高吞吐、高可用性和公平性等优势。然而，拜占庭容错模型下的快速SMR研究相对不足，现有协议数量稀少，缺乏对复制因子的系统性理解。

Method: 针对拜占庭容错快速状态机复制问题，从理论层面推导复制因子的上界和下界，证明其紧确界；同时设计实现了一个次优协议，用于展示复制因子与恢复效率之间的权衡关系。

Result: 首次建立了拜占庭容错快速状态机复制协议中复制因子的紧确上下界；提出了一个次优协议实例，揭示了复制因子与恢复效率之间存在的设计权衡空间。

Conclusion: 本文为拜占庭容错快速状态机复制协议的理论基础做出了重要贡献，明确了复制因子的最优边界，并展示了协议设计中的权衡可能性，为后续BFT协议的优化设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Fast state-machine replication (SMR) protocols in the crash-fault setting have attracted significant interest in both academia and industry. This interest stems from their advantages over leader-based protocols, including low execution latency for non-conflicting commands, high throughput, improved availability, and increased fairness. While Fast SMR is well studied in the crash-fault setting, the Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) setting remains much less understood, with only a small number of existing Fast BFT SMR protocols. In this paper, we present tight upper and lower bounds on the replication factor required for Fast BFT SMR. We also present a suboptimal protocol that illustrates a trade-off between replication factor and recovery efficiency.

</details>


### [80] [CacheRoute: Planned Prefix-Affinity Routing for Large-Scale LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19677)
*Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CacheRoute通过周期性路由计划解决前缀缓存中的缓存感知与负载均衡权衡，在Llama-3.3-70B上实现176 QPS，相比基线提升2.3倍，KV缓存命中率从64.1%提升至93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存仅在请求返回到仍持有前缀KV的服务器时才能避免预填充阶段。现有的缓存无感知负载均衡会分散请求复用，而固定亲和性虽能保留复用但可能导致服务器过载，存在明显的权衡困境。

Method: CacheRoute采用周期性路由计划，将高频请求键接入稳定的预热集合并根据预期负载进行分配。高热度键可使用多个目的地，同时通过半合成聚合实验和控制实验分离亲和性与放置策略的影响。

Result: 在60块H100 GPU上运行fp8量化的Llama-3.3-70B模型，CacheRoute在3.5秒P99 SLO下维持176±11 QPS，是五个基线中最强者的2.3倍。服务端KV缓存命中率从缓存无感知负载均衡下的64.1±1.3%提升至93.2±0.5%。

Conclusion: 当亲和性恢复的KV工作量不足时，残留的负载偏斜会减少甚至消除改进效果。建议在部署前使用影子回放进行验证，而非仅依赖工作负载统计信息启用亲和性机制。

Abstract: Prefix caching avoids prefill only when a repeated request returns to a server that still holds the prefix KV. Cache-blind balancing disperses that reuse; fixed affinity preserves it but can overload a server. CacheRoute resolves this tradeoff with a periodic routing plan. It admits high-rate keys to a stable warm set and places their assignments by expected load. Hot keys may use more than one destination, although every key in our primary semi-synthetic aggregate uses exactly one. On Llama-3.3-70B in fp8 across 60 H100 GPUs, CacheRoute sustains 176+/-11 QPS at a 3.5-s p99 SLO, 2.3x the strongest of five baselines. Served KV-cache hit rate rises from 64.1+/-1.3% under cache-blind balancing to 93.2+/-0.5%. A second semi-synthetic aggregate and controlled 8B and burst experiments separate the effects of affinity and placement. Two 32B workloads provide the counterexamples: when affinity recovers too little KV work, its residual load skew reduces or erases the improvement. We therefore recommend gating any deployment with a shadow replay rather than enabling affinity from workload statistics alone.

</details>


### [81] [ParaWeb: Parallel Programming Patterns for Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19935)
*Suejb Memeti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ParaWeb是一个TypeScript库，为Web环境实现了10种并行编程模式，提供消息传递、共享缓冲区和GPU三种实现变体。在计算密集型任务中，CPU版本最高加速11.6倍（16线程），GPU版本最高加速260倍，图像卷积案例中GPU加速可达414倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用需要计算密集型处理，但JavaScript传统上受限于单线程执行模型。虽然Node.js Worker Threads和浏览器Web Workers提供了底层并行执行机制，但开发者缺乏捕获可复用并行结构的高级抽象模式。

Method: 开发了ParaWeb TypeScript库，实现了10种并行编程模式，支持服务端Node.js、客户端浏览器和WebGPU计算着色器。每种模式提供三种实现变体：基于postMessage的消息传递(MP)变体、使用SharedArrayBuffer的共享缓冲区(Shared)变体、以及使用WebGPU计算着色器的GPU变体。详细描述了架构、设计决策和模式特定的实现策略，并在三种数据规模上评估了全部30个实现的性能。

Result: CPU变体在计算密集型模式下使用16线程实现最高11.6倍加速；GPU变体在Farm、Scatter、Reduce和Map等高算术强度的计算密集型模式下实现最高260倍加速。在五个图像卷积滤波器的案例研究中，对于不可分离核，GPU加速相比单线程CPU达到最高414倍加速，在1024×1024、2048×2048和4K图像上保持一致的扩展性。

Conclusion: ParaWeb成功地为Web环境提供了高级并行编程抽象，通过多种实现策略显著提升了计算性能，证明了GPU加速在Web计算密集型应用中的巨大潜力，为开发者提供了可复用的并行编程模式库。

Abstract: Modern web applications increasingly require computationally intensive processing, yet JavaScript, the dominant language of the web, has traditionally been limited to a single-threaded execution model. Node.js Worker Threads and browser Web Workers provide low-level mechanisms for parallel execution, but developers lack high-level abstractions that capture recurring parallel structures as reusable patterns. In this paper, we present ParaWeb, a TypeScript library that implements ten parallel programming patterns for server-side Node.js, client-side browser environments, and WebGPU compute shaders. ParaWeb provides three implementation variants for each pattern: a message-passing (MP) variant based on structured cloning via postMessage, a shared-buffer (Shared) variant that uses SharedArrayBuffer with typed array views, and a GPU variant that uses WebGPU compute shaders for hardware-accelerated execution. We describe the architecture, design decisions, and pattern-specific implementation strategies, and we evaluate the performance of all thirty implementations across three data sizes. Experimental evaluation results show that the CPU-based variants achieve speedups of up to 11.6x with 16 threads for compute-bound patterns, while the GPU variants reach speedups of up to 260x for compute-bound patterns with high arithmetic intensity such as Farm, Scatter, Reduce, and Map. A case study on five image-convolution filters further shows that GPU acceleration reaches up to 414x speedup over single-threaded CPU on non-separable kernels, with consistent scaling across 1024x1024$, 2048x2048$, and 4K images.

</details>


### [82] [Design and Empirical Evaluation of a Network-Centric, On-Premises Architecture for Earth Observation Data Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20283)
*João Pinelo,João Gonçalves,Denis Willett,Amit Ruhela,Derek Steinmoeller,Uriel Mendoza,Pelumi S. Alao,Ronald Soares Lopes,Rogerio Atem de Carvalho,Pedro Mattos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在机构网络受限情况下，如何构建本地部署的地球观测数据访问架构。通过在实际部署的AIR数据中心进行测试，发现网络带宽是影响存储吞吐量的主要瓶颈，但当带宽超过10 Gbps/服务器时，端点内存拓扑成为关键制约因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测项目产生的数据量巨大，超过了大多数机构网络的传输和存储能力。虽然公有云平台可以解决资源充足组织的问题，但大西洋盆地的机构面临网络连接、数据主权和资金限制，使得本地基础设施成为唯一可行方案。云原生数据格式支持高效的局部读取，但其性能依赖于底层网络架构的带宽，这种依赖性很少被单独测量。

Method: 构建了一个可复制的、以网络为中心的本地部署EO数据访问架构，在AIR数据中心（Atlantic Cloud创始节点）进行了首次运营部署评估。系统包括：基于100 GbE架构的MinIO对象存储集群、PostGIS元数据目录和OGC API-EDR访问层。通过在持续并行负载下表征网络架构，评估对象存储在EO代表性工作负载下的吞吐量，并与相同硬件上的节流基线进行对比，将网络带宽作为唯一变量进行隔离分析。同时与合作伙伴机构进行多站点复制基准测试。

Result: 网络带宽是批量EO数据访问中存储吞吐量的主要约束，这一约束在一个阈值范围内有效；超过该阈值后，端点内存拓扑（而非容量）决定了系统能使用的带宽数量。对于所测试的硬件级别，该阈值高于每台服务器10 Gbps。低于此阈值时，仅网络容量就决定了设施能够提供的服务；高于此阈值时，进一步网络投资的回报取决于端点内存配置，后者可以延后购买。

Conclusion: 网络带宽是本地部署EO数据访问系统的关键制约因素，在带宽资源有限的情况下应优先投资网络基础设施。当带宽充足（>10 Gbps/服务器）时，需要关注端点内存拓扑优化。该架构为受连接、主权和资金限制的机构提供了可行的本地部署方案，支持多站点联邦复制。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) programmes generate data at volumes that exceed the transfer and storage capacity of most institutional networks. Public cloud platforms address this for well-resourced organisations, but institutions across the Atlantic basin face constraints in connectivity, sovereignty and funding that make on-premises infrastructure the only viable path. Cloud-native data formats enable efficient partial reads, yet their performance depends on the bandwidth of the underlying network fabric, a dependency rarely measured in isolation. This paper presents a replicable, network-centric architecture for on-premises EO data access, evaluated at its first operational deployment: the AIR Data Centre, founding node of the Atlantic Cloud. The system comprises a MinIO object storage cluster on a 100 GbE fabric, a PostGIS metadata catalogue and an OGC API-EDR access layer. We characterise the fabric under sustained parallel load, evaluate object storage throughput for EO-representative workloads, and compare measured performance against throttled baselines on identical hardware, isolating network bandwidth as the sole variable. Multi-site replication benchmarks with partner institutions characterise the federation primitive the model depends on. Network bandwidth is the dominant constraint on storage throughput for bulk EO data access up to a threshold; beyond it, endpoint memory topology rather than capacity governs how much bandwidth a system can use. For this hardware class that threshold lies above 10 Gbps per server. Below it, network capacity alone sets what the facility can deliver; above it, the return on further network investment depends on endpoint memory provisioning, which can be deferred and bought later.

</details>

### [83] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于长短期记忆(LSTM)循环神经网络的监督学习模型，用于估算直升机起飞重量，并在传统航电计算机上演示了符合EASA机器学习应用概念和Eurocae ED-324标准的实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算直升机起飞重量对机载告警等关键功能至关重要，传统的重量估算方法可能存在精度不足的问题。本研究旨在利用空客全球在役机队的海量数据集，开发一种新的机器学习模型来提高重量估算的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 采用监督学习方法，使用长短期记忆(LSTM)循环神经网络作为模型架构；建立了机器学习需求集、模型描述及其实现方案；遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件和Eurocae ED-324标准设计了学习保障流程；在传统航电计算机上进行实现验证。

Result: 成功实现了符合航空安全标准的机器学习重量估算模型；验证了需求与实现的一致性；在传统航电计算机上证明了模型部署的可行性，适用于机载告警等关键功能应用。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了机器学习模型在航空关键系统中应用的可行性，为后续在机载目标上部署机器学习模型奠定了技术基础，同时提供了符合航空安全认证要求的完整开发流程参考。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [84] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三角模糊重缩放距离(d_{TR})，一种在距离计算中直接集成线性重缩放的度量方法，解决了异质属性模糊数据比较时的尺度不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统决策中常使用三角模糊数(TFNs)处理不精确或不确定信息，而现有的TFN距离度量方法假设所有值在同一尺度上，应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异质属性时需要预先标准化，这给实际应用带来了不便。

Method: 提出三角模糊重缩放距离(d_{TR})，将线性重缩放(LRE)直接集成到距离计算中，在比较模糊数的过程中实现标准化。引入权重向量以优先考虑不同维度，并形式化证明其满足度量属性。

Result: 形式化证明了d_{TR}满足度量属性(非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式)，并证明了其有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，这些性质结合权重向量使其适用于异质模糊数据应用。

Conclusion: d_{TR}度量方法成功解决了异质模糊数据的距离度量问题，适用于构建综合指标、基于距离的机器学习算法以及多准则决策辅助等应用场景。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [85] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Holtercare-23K大规模动态ECG多模态数据集和Holtercare-Bench基准测试，填补了动态心电图领域缺乏高质量数据集和评估基准的空白。研究发现当前MLLM在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距，但通过微调可以获得实质性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在医学应用中主要针对静态图像或短期信号，在动态心电图这一关键领域，由于缺乏高质量数据集和基准测试，模型在复杂时间推理和诊断报告生成方面表现不佳，亟需专门针对动态ECG的数据集和评估框架。

Method: 构建了Holtercare-23K数据集，包含22,980个QA对，来源于788份临床Holter记录，采用创新的信号-视频-文本三模态对齐方式；基于此数据集开发了Holtercare-Bench基准测试，涵盖时间定位、临床诊断和全局摘要三个评估维度；对领先的MLLM进行零样本评估和微调实验。

Result: 零样本评估显示领先MLLM在处理超长病理序列时存在显著性能差距；微调代表性模型后获得了实质性性能提升；揭示了当前MLLM在电生理学领域的局限性。

Conclusion: 本研究为长期医疗MLLM提供了基础性基准，指出了当前多模态大语言模型在电生理学领域的不足，为未来该领域的研究发展奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [86] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索了量子-经典混合机器学习方法，利用cfDNA片段组学和甲基化数据进行肺癌检测，部分量子核模型在片段组学数据上表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌低剂量CT筛查存在依从性差和管理困难等局限。基于血液的cfDNA生物标志物可提供补充方案，但肺癌异质性和高维非线性分子信号使早期检测仍具挑战性，需要新方法来有效处理这些复杂信号。

Method: 采用量子-经典混合机器学习方法，基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化数据进行肺癌检测。经特征筛选后使用20和40特征子集训练模型，通过角度和密集角度特征图将特征编码至量子希尔伯特空间，采用多种纠缠策略，计算基于保真度的量子核，并与传统SVM模型进行对比评估。

Result: 片段组学数据中，多个20特征量子核模型AUC优于传统SVM基线，表明能有效捕获cfDNA断裂的非线性结构；甲基化数据中传统SVM表现最佳，但部分量子模型保持竞争力并在特异度上有提升；特征从20增至40未能持续改善性能，反而增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法在基于cfDNA的肺癌检测中展现出良好前景，能够有效处理高维非线性分子信号，为液体活检提供新的技术路径。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [87] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用目标数据集构建简单分类器的方法，通过结合现有不确定性分数和相似查询的正确性特征来预测LLM响应的正确性，实现了比现有零样本方法更优的不确定性量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型不确定性量化方法（如语言化置信度和多重生成方法）通常是零样本的，虽然无需标签数据即可产生不确定性分数，但在实际部署前仍需要在特定数据集上评估其性能。作者观察到这一评估过程可以利用目标数据集来提升不确定性量化的性能。

Method: 利用目标数据集构建简单分类器来预测LLM响应的正确性。该方法将现有的不确定性分数和相似查询的正确性作为特征输入，训练分类器进行预测。该方法产生的计算开销极小，是一种低成本、直接的不确定性量化增强方法。

Result: 通过利用目标数据集，所提出的方法在各种设置下始终优于现有的零样本不确定性量化分数。该方法仅需最小的计算开销即可实现性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法为实际应用中的LLM不确定性量化提供了一种低成本且直接的增强方案，证明了利用目标数据集进行有监督学习能够有效提升不确定性量化的性能，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [88] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"机制层析成像"(mechanistic tomography)框架，将可解释性研究中的各种测量方法统一为线性测量形式y=Ax+w，为从模型内部恢复机制和干预效果提供了系统化的方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究中的各种方法(如patching、梯度、Hessian-vector products等)在不同访问假设下测量不同的目标量，缺乏统一的测量结构理解。需要一个统一的框架来理解这些方法的共同结构，并提供实用的测量程序。

Method: 将测量统一为线性形式y=Ax+w，其中A描述干预，x是目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基函数误配。提出实用程序：从成本最低的测量开始，在目标规模上用留出干预测试，校准简单失配，当存在结构化残差时扩展测量族。在多个模型(HMM、GPT-2-small IOI、Qwen-2.5-7B)和不同访问条件下验证方法。

Result: 在两HMM模型中，控制误差随观测者误差增加；在仅前向访问下，稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching用更少干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下，有限探针改进局部归因映射；提升测量和Hessian-vector products恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互；在GPT-2-small IOI上，Name Mover-Negative Name Mover交互是三个测试跨组对中最大的留出预测项；在Qwen-2.5-7B上，有限校准使加性拒绝响应映射充分，留出误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制层析成像框架成功统一了可解释性测量方法，提供了实用的测量程序和验证方法。通过控制任务验证了方法的有效性，展示了在不同访问条件和模型上的应用效果，为可解释性研究提供了系统化的方法论基础。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [89] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了神经网络鲁棒性验证中基于模板加速技术的有效性及其局限性，提出联合稳定神经元指标解释性能差异，并开发了FastCert技术实现模板的自动分配以提升验证速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于模板的证明共享技术已被证明能加速神经网络的非完备验证，但该技术在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的鲁棒性尚不清楚。研究其有效性边界对于实际应用至关重要。

Method: 1) 对基于模板加速技术进行系统性研究，分析模板包含率在不同场景下的变化；2) 提出联合稳定神经元这一新指标来解释性能差异；3) 开发FastCert技术，能够自动将模板分配到神经网络各层，当模板无法提供加速时完全避免使用。

Result: 模板包含率在不同场景下差异显著；联合稳定神经元指标能解释这种变化；FastCert技术在大规模基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务上，相比现有模板重用技术平均实现了1.13倍加速。

Conclusion: 基于模板的加速技术效果受场景影响较大，通过联合稳定神经元指标可预测其有效性，FastCert能够智能地分配或舍弃模板以优化验证性能，为神经网络鲁棒性验证提供了更可靠的加速方案。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [90] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)的纵向贝叶斯学习框架，用于从纵向扩散张量成像(DTI)数据中连续估计阿尔茨海默病的严重程度，并生成Disease Continuum Score (DCS)来量化个体在疾病连续体中的位置及其不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病是一个连续的生物过程，但现有的基于神经影像的人工智能方法大多局限于横断面成像的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法有效捕捉疾病的连续性进展特征。

Method: 提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)纵向贝叶斯学习框架，将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜在变量，通过联合整合纵向观察和弱临床监督来估计疾病连续体位置，并由此推导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)及其相关不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明，DCP始终优于代表性疾病进展方法。验证分析显示DCS能准确表征疾病严重程度，具有强临床相关性，保留纵向疾病演化特征，并能预测未来疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种定量成像衍生表示方法，能够超越传统诊断标签和临床评分，实现对阿尔茨海默病进展的连续评估。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [91] [FleetSieve: Decision-Critical Profiling for SLO-Aware LLM Fleet Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19659)
*Huang Cheng,Scott Zhang,Aubert Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FleetSieve是一种用于大型语言模型服务集群配置选择的智能测量方法，通过联合建模容量和尾部延迟，有选择性地进行性能分析，相比随机采样平均节省5.4%的GPU时间，并有效避免违反SLO的配置选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型服务集群的张量并行(TP)度选择和副本数量配置存在非单调性能特征，可行配置随负载变化而改变。穷尽式性能分析虽然能解决这种不确定性，但会测量许多不影响最终资源分配的配置，造成计算资源浪费。

Method: FleetSieve根据测量对资源耦合、SLO感知的集群决策的预期影响来选择测量；联合建模容量和尾部延迟；比较保守和乐观的资源配置方案；当剩余决策差距低于指定容忍度时停止测量过程。

Result: 在31B参数模型和H100测量网格上，FleetSieve使用22,200 GPU-秒达到oracle决策，比随机采样节省6.9%；在200次随机揭示顺序下平均节省5.4% GPU时间；在Chat工作负载上节省21.5%；联合建模成功避免选择违反30秒SLO的配置（其p99延迟为46.4秒）。

Conclusion: FleetSieve证明了基于决策影响的智能测量选择能够有效减少配置分析开销，联合容量和尾部延迟建模对于满足SLO约束至关重要，负载相关的尾部延迟机制通过边界重复和BurstGPT测量得到验证。

Abstract: Choosing tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and replica counts for an LLM serving fleet is difficult because performance is not monotonic in TP and the feasible choice can change with load. Exhaustive profiling resolves this uncertainty, but measures many configurations that do not affect the final resource allocation. We present FleetSieve, which selects measurements according to their expected effect on a resource-coupled, SLO-aware fleet decision. FleetSieve models capacity and tail latency jointly, compares conservative and optimistic allocations, and stops when their remaining decision gap is below a specified tolerance. On a fixed H100 measurement grid for a 31B-parameter open-weight model, FleetSieve reaches the oracle aggregate decision using 22,200 GPU-seconds, 6.9% less than uniform random profiling in the fixed comparison. Across 200 random reveal orders, its mean saving over random profiling is 5.4% (95% bootstrap CI: 3.5-7.2%). The fixed-comparison saving is 21.5% for Chat, while FleetSieve does not use the fewest GPU-seconds for Code. Joint capacity and tail modeling also avoids selecting a configuration whose 46.4-second completion p99 violates a 30-second SLO. In a 16-GPU allocation, an incorrect sparse-profile decision loses up to 1.93 requests/s and 12.4 percentage points of max-min fulfillment. Boundary repeats and BurstGPT measurements support the observed load-dependent tail-latency mechanism.

</details>


### [92] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EventTime多分辨率框架，结合长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据，通过事件融合模块和动态对比目标，有效预测网络安全事件披露后的短期异常损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件披露等突发冲击会严重扰乱金融时间序列并导致巨额异常损失。然而现有时间序列预测模型主要关注内生规律（趋势、季节性、自相关），难以处理罕见且异质的外部事件，同时稀疏的高影响事件和背景市场噪声进一步增加了预测难度。

Method: 提出EventTime多分辨率框架，整合长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据；设计事件融合模块将时间表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关市场模式；引入动态对比目标构建事件和时间序列感知的正负样本对以缓解稀疏监督问题；构建SECURE真实数据集，对齐网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列及结构化/语义特征。

Result: EventTime在估计事件后金融损失方面持续优于最先进的时间序列和事件感知基线模型；分析表明该方法能学习更具事件敏感性的表示，对不完整元数据具有更强的鲁棒性，并能提供更可解释的短期市场影响估计。

Conclusion: EventTime框架成功解决了事件条件下的市场冲击预测问题，为网络安全事件披露后的短期异常损失估计提供了有效解决方案，在实际应用中具有良好的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [93] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力进行表格数据异常检测，无需微调或神经网络训练，在24个数据集上优于15个SOTA基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据异常检测具有挑战性，异常样本往往源于特征间依赖关系的违反而非简单的边缘分布偏差。现有检测器依赖几何或重构信号，而基于LLM的方法主要进行正常样本微调或合成异常生成，存在局限性。

Method: 将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果依赖关系和蒸馏原型，组织成提示用于代码生成。生成的评分引擎评估统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常性，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。利用LLM的上下文学习能力实现结构化、提示条件的评分合成。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上评估，与15个SOTA基线方法比较，在混合类型和纯连续型数据设置中均显示一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该设计无需LLM微调或神经网络训练，降低了计算成本，实现了实用的现实世界表格系统异常检测。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [94] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对概念漂移下的模型再训练策略，本文通过大规模实证研究发现：增量学习比再训练策略选择更关键。有增量学习时，不同策略与不重新训练无显著差异；无增量学习时，策略选择导致15-55个百分点准确率差距，周期性重训练在突变和渐进漂移下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的机器学习系统受概念漂移影响会导致性能下降，但从业者在何时重新训练方面缺乏原则性指导。重新训练成本高昂且预算有限，训练和部署延迟导致旧模型在数据持续变化期间仍在服务，这些问题促使研究不同模型刷新策略的有效性。

Method: 在统一系统模型下进行受控实证研究，对比三种实际模型刷新策略（周期性重训练、错误阈值触发、ADWIN统计漂移触发重训练）与不重训练基线。实验覆盖3933次运行，跨越三种漂移模式、三种预算水平、多达五种延迟水平、三个数据集和两种学习模式。

Result: 最关键的设计决策不是重训练策略而是是否采用增量学习。对于具有即时标签的线性在线学习器，增量更新使所有策略与基线无实际显著差异（54组配对比较）。无增量学习时，策略选择导致15-55个百分点的漂移后准确率差距：周期性重训练在突变和渐进漂移下显著优于反应式策略，反应式策略仅在循环漂移下保持优势。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了反应式策略的系统性失效模式和延迟-预算队列交互问题（使有效重训练预算减半），发布了完整模拟器、数据管道和每次运行产物以确保可重现性。研究表明增量学习比策略选择更为根本，为生产环境ML系统的模型维护提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [95] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeltaMomentum通过方向感知的动量更新机制，使每个方向的遗忘率与其出现频率匹配，在FineWeb-Edu预训练中将AdamW收敛速度提升最高46.39%，且计算开销仅为线性层成本的22.2%-25.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化器使用固定遗忘率的指数移动平均(EMA)构建动量，但深度网络训练中输入分布高度各向异性，导致少数方向频繁出现而大多数方向罕见。现有方法仅在动量缓冲区外增加额外处理，未改变动量更新本身，无法有效解决各向异性问题。

Method: 利用线性层梯度可分解为输入侧(作为key)和输出侧误差(作为value)的结构，DeltaMomentum将标准delta规则应用于动量缓冲区更新。每个方向的遗忘率由其出现频率决定，而非固定遗忘率。该方法可作为任何优化器的动量缓冲区的即插即用替代方案，在μP下系数可跨宽度迁移，无需持久内存，额外计算量仅为gated-MLP块线性层成本的22.2%-25.0%。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，AdamW+DeltaMomentum(DeltaAdamW)相比AdamW在67M规模达到相同验证损失需要减少46.39±4.32%的训练步数，370M规模减少22.12±0.80%，且该增益在Chinchilla最优预算下的1B规模依然保持。方法对SGD、ResNet-18和ViT-Tiny在CIFAR-10上也有效。训练时诊断证实了更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum成功将方向感知能力内置到动量更新规则中，理论上证明了其作为有效动量的性质，能够应用输入侧曲率校正而无需矩阵求逆，在固定和漂移最优点下比EMA更快清除过时方向，为深度学习优化提供了一种计算高效且理论有据的动量改进方案。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出DBOSC方法测试多模态世界模型是否共享可执行意义，发现虽然不同模态表示在响应空间中具有可替换性，但在有序执行层面存在严格限制，揭示了属性访问、响应替换、融合闭合和有序执行是四个独立的可测试能力层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型将紧凑多模态表示作为感知与物理交互的接口，但缺乏探测方法验证不同传感器是否携带相同的可执行意义，以及该意义是否能在新的动作组合中保持。需要建立操作性能力层次结构来系统评估多模态表示的语义一致性和执行可靠性。

Method: 引入操作性能力层次结构和DBOSC（不相交-桥接算子替换证书）方法，测试独立训练的模态编译器是否能在训练面板之外根据证据互换进入冻结响应图表。在Cluster Haptic数据集上测试音频和加速度表示，并在受控弹塑性系统中测试有序执行能力。采用预注册预算、收敛预算对比实验设计。

Result: 在Cluster Haptic上，同一未见表面的音频和加速度表示在响应空间中比错误配对接近4.5倍，19个保持表面均成立；弹塑性系统中，预注册预算下执行器拒绝执行，收敛预算下秩为3的图表成功执行（oracle NMSE 0.18），16项检查中14项通过；发出完整程序的执行器比实体盲预测器差38倍；融合在双模态上均有提升，两个失败源于融合信息矩阵对角限制与全矩阵表现相当。

Conclusion: 研究将属性访问、响应替换、融合闭合和有序执行分离为四个独立且可单独测试的能力层级。压缩和融合本身无法确定未见组合律，存在非可识别性结果。执行门控清除是执行器的属性而非图表属性，为多模态世界模型的能力评估提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart. A matching non-identifiability result explains why compression and fusion alone cannot determine an unseen composition law. These results separate attribute access, response substitution, fusion closure, and ordered execution into distinct, separately testable achievements.

</details>


### [97] [Empirical Characterization of Learning Geometry in Hybrid Quantum Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19497)
*Sandra Leticia Juárez-Osorio,Jorge I. Hernandez-Martinez,Jesus Ivan Ruiz-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过神经切线核(NTK)动力学分析，发现紧凑型混合量子预测模型与经典基线模型在相似性能下展现出显著不同的学习轨迹，揭示了架构依赖性学习行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注模型最终性能，缺乏对混合量子-经典模型学习动力学过程的深入理解。研究旨在通过NTK分析揭示不同架构在学习过程中的内在差异，而不仅仅是比较端点准确率。

Method: 使用平稳谐波混合和非平稳chirp基准测试，通过核-目标对齐、核漂移、谱集中度和训练损失等指标分析NTK动力学。设置经典基线、混合量子模型和傅里叶增强基线进行对比，并通过重上传消融实验控制变量。

Result: 混合量子模型参数量仅为经典模型的44%(125 vs 281)，在18个频率条件中的15个更快达到验证检查点。经典模型早期目标对齐更强，混合模型核谱集中度更低且核漂移更小。两者最终性能相近，傅里叶增强基线无法复现混合模型行为。

Conclusion: 相当程度的泛化能力可以从本质上不同的学习轨迹中产生，单个NTK诊断指标无法单调预测验证收敛性。研究揭示了被最终准确率掩盖的架构依赖性学习行为，而非宣称通用的量子优势。

Abstract: We characterize the learning dynamics of a compact hybrid quantum forecasting model through comparison with a structurally aligned classical baseline. Using stationary harmonic-mixture and nonstationary chirp benchmarks with controlled spectral complexity and data availability, we analyze empirical Neural Tangent Kernel dynamics through kernel-target alignment, kernel drift, spectral concentration, and training loss. The classical model exhibits stronger early target alignment, whereas the hybrid model generally develops a less concentrated kernel spectrum and smaller kernel drift. Despite these distinct optimization geometries, both architectures attain similar held-out performance across the evaluated regimes. Notably, the hybrid model uses 125 trainable parameters compared with 281 for the classical baseline and reaches its validation-selected checkpoint earlier in 15 of 18 frequency conditions. A Fourier-augmented classical baseline does not reproduce the observed training behavior, while a controlled re-uploading ablation shows that repeated encoding systematically modifies both optimization and kernel geometry. These results demonstrate that comparable generalization can emerge from substantially different learning trajectories and that individual NTK diagnostics do not provide monotonic predictors of validation convergence. Rather than claiming a general quantum advantage, the study identifies architecture-dependent learning behavior that is masked by endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [98] [In Two Minds about Lifelong Learning: Exploring Hemispheric Redundancy and Specialisation in Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19514)
*Benjamin Smith,Levin Kuhlmann,Kaushik Roy,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了4MAS架构，模拟生物大脑的不对称半球、长短时记忆机制和睡眠过程，以解决机器学习中的持续学习问题，在多个基准数据集上取得了有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习系统在持续学习方面面临灾难性遗忘的挑战，无法像生物系统那样在不遗忘旧知识的情况下学习新知识。当原始训练数据不可用时，传统的全数据重训练方法变得不切实际。因此，作者受到生物动物能够持续学习且不发生灾难性遗忘的启发，试图构建一个模拟生物记忆巩固机制的框架。

Method: 提出了4MAS（4 Module Awake/Sleep）宏观架构，该架构基于三个生物学概念：经验回放、REM睡眠和大脑半球不对称性。模型包含两个不对称的半球，每个半球具有独立的长时记忆和短时记忆机制，并在增量学习任务之间引入睡眠阶段以促进记忆巩固。这种设计模拟了生物大脑在清醒和睡眠状态下的不同记忆处理过程。

Result: 在Split-MNIST、Split-Fashion-MNIST和Split-CIFAR-100三个持续学习基准数据集上分别取得了98.3%、84.9%和29.29%的分类准确率，达到了与现有方法相当的性能水平，证明了该生物学启发架构在缓解灾难性遗忘方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过模拟生物大脑的结构特征和记忆巩固过程，特别是引入不对称半球设计和睡眠机制，可以有效改善机器学习模型的持续学习能力。这项工作为构建具有类脑学习能力的持久智能系统提供了新的设计思路和实践验证。

Abstract: Persistent intelligent systems require the ability to learn continually, but current machine learning approaches face significant challenges in this area compared to biological learning systems. Machine learning algorithms typically trade off retention of previously learned information and adaptation to new or changing data patterns. When continual learning capabilities are absent, algorithms must undergo retraining using the entire data set, an approach that becomes impractical when original training data are unavailable due to storage constraints, financial or computational costs, or privacy restrictions. However, biological animals can learn continually, without experiencing catastrophic forgetting. This paper attempts to build a high-level framework for how animals learn and preserve knowledge by modelling neural components and states that are known to be related to memory consolidation. We focus on three concepts: experience replay, REM sleep, and bilaterality. We propose 4MAS (4 Module Awake/Sleep), a novel macroarchitecture demonstrating how machine learning models might benefit from asymmetric hemispheres, each with their own long- and short-term memory mechanisms, and how a period of sleep between incremental learning tasks might benefit memory consolidation. Finally, we present results showing that our architecture achieves competitive results on the Split-MNIST, Split-Fashion-MNIST and Split-CIFAR-100 datasets, with 98.3%, 84.9%, and 29.29% accuracy respectively.

</details>


### [99] [Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19540)
*Yara Bahram,Zahra Dehghani,Mélodie Desbos,Eric Granger,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MeanFlow-Transfer方法，统一了不同参数化预训练模型的领域自适应与加速，并结合连续对抗性MeanFlow后训练阶段，在极少采样步数下实现高质量生成，NFE减少最多125倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两个关键挑战：（1）预训练扩散/流模型适应新领域时，多步采样成本高且现有加速方法依赖于特定参数化（ε、x、v、u），缺乏统一的加速目标；（2）对抗性精化仅适用于瞬时速度流模型，无法直接应用于预测有限区间平均速度的MeanFlow模型。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：第一阶段为MeanFlow-Transfer，将异构源模型输出映射到共享速度表示，用于初始化MeanFlow生成器并优化目标领域MF目标，在单一训练循环中统一自适应与加速；第二阶段为连续对抗性MeanFlow（CAMF），扩展连续对抗流模型到MF的有限区间平均速度，通过对比真实与预测区间端点的学习势能变化恢复细节。

Result: 在四个基于ImageNet的源模型（DiT-ε、SiT-v、JiT-x、iMF-u）适应五个目标领域的实验中，MF-T结合CAMF在FID和FDD指标上匹配或超过微调教师模型，同时NFE减少最多125倍；CAMF平均改善MF-T少步FID达29%。

Conclusion: MeanFlow-Transfer成功统一了异构预训练模型的领域自适应与加速问题，CAMF有效解决了对抗性精化在有限区间平均速度模型中的应用限制，两者结合实现了跨多种预训练模型的高效少步高质量生成。

Abstract: Training fast generators on new domains with limited data remains challenging for two reasons. First, adapting a pretrained diffusion or flow model to a new domain leaves its costly multi-step sampling unaddressed, and existing acceleration methods are tied to the source parameterization--$ε$, $x$, $v$, or $u$--leaving heterogeneous pretrained models with no common acceleration target. Second, while adversarial refinement is proven effective for few-step quality, it is formulated only for instantaneous-velocity flows, not for the finite-interval average velocities that MeanFlow (MF) models predict. We address both problems. We propose MeanFlow-Transfer, which maps heterogeneous source outputs into a shared velocity representation, uses it to initialize an MF generator from the source weights, and optimizes an MF objective on the target domain. This unifies adaptation and acceleration in a single training loop across a broad range of pretrained models. We then introduce Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow, a post-training stage that extends continuous adversarial flow models from instantaneous velocities to MF's finite-interval average velocities. CAMF contrasts changes in a learned potential between real and predicted interval endpoints, recovering fine detail that MF regression averages away, and reduces to the instantaneous criterion in the vanishing-interval limit. Adapting four ImageNet-based source models--DiT ($ε$), SiT ($v$), JiT ($x$), iMF ($u$)--to five target domains, MF-T with CAMF matches or exceeds the fine-tuned teacher in FID and FDD at up to $125\times$ fewer Neural Function Evaluations (NFEs), while CAMF improves MF-T's few-step FID by $29\%$ on average.

</details>


### [100] [DraftFM: A FoundationModel for Day-Zero Drafting in Magic: The Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19568)
*Brian Ward*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DraftFM是一种针对Magic: The Gathering新扩展包零日预测的离散选择策略，能够在未见过的卡牌扩展上实现约50-60%的选牌准确率，并在《霍比特人》扩展包上生成的卡牌排名与专家评审结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型依赖历史选牌数据进行训练，无法处理尚未发布的新扩展包。研究旨在解决零日场景下的卡牌评估问题——即在没有任何选牌记录的情况下，仅凭公开卡牌信息预测玩家选牌行为。

Method: 提出DraftFM模型，将每张卡牌编码为775维特征向量（包含公开记录、结构化特征和固定文本嵌入），不使用卡牌身份、扩展包身份或使用统计信息。采用160万参数网络，在29个扩展包的1.49亿次人类选牌记录上训练，使用离散选择策略对当前牌包中的可用卡牌进行评分。

Result: 在完全保留的三个扩展包上达到50.8%、60.4%、56.7%的top-1预测准确率（基准均匀随机约为7%）。对未发布的《霍比特人》扩展包生成的卡牌排名与六位独立专家评审的一致性程度，相当于专家之间的一致性水平。

Conclusion: DraftFM证明了纯特征驱动的模型能够在零日场景下有效预测选牌行为，无需依赖身份或历史统计信息即可泛化至未见过的扩展包，为新扩展包的发布前评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Drafting a new Magic: The Gathering expansion begins before any pick from it has been observed: the complete card list is public, but the draft logs that supervised pick models train on do not yet exist. We study this day-zero regime directly. DraftFM is a discrete-choice policy that scores exactly the cards available in the current pack, conditioned on the drafted pool and the state of the draft. Every card enters as a frozen 775-dimensional function of its public card record, structured features and a fixed text embedding, with no card identities, set identities, or usage statistics anywhere in the model, so an unseen card is scored by the same machinery as a familiar one. A 1.6-million-parameter network fitted on 149 million human picks from 29 expansions predicts held-out picks in three expansions withheld in their entirety, reaching 50.8%, 60.4%, and 56.7% top-1 agreement, where uniform chance at the opening pick is about 7%. Refitted on all 32 observed expansions, the same architecture produced a card ranking for the then-unreleased set The Hobbit, sealed with its complete cryptographic provenance and published roughly 36 hours before the set became draftable on MTG Arena. The sealed ranking agrees with six independent expert reviewers roughly as much as those reviewers agree with one another. Evaluation against realized outcomes is committed to a follow-on note, whatever it shows.

</details>


### [101] [A Two-Stage Time-Aware Transformer for Short-Horizon AECOPD Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19578)
*Dongyang Wang,Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于家庭呼吸机原始压力和流量波形的两阶段框架，实现AECOPD急性加重风险分类和发生时间预测，分类F1达0.91，时间预测RMSE为1.00天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD病情恶化迅速，及时预测是临床优先事项。现有机器学习方法依赖间断收集的临床变量，引入延迟，限制家庭监测实用性。家庭呼吸机提供低延迟替代方案，但现有方法要么压缩波形为手工特征，要么仅关注二元风险分类，无法预测事件发生时间。

Method: 两阶段框架直接处理家庭呼吸机最近7天的原始压力和流量波形。第一阶段分类模型识别严重加重高风险患者，第二阶段回归模型估计距离事件发生的剩余天数。

Result: 两阶段模型在风险分类和时间预测上均优于传统基线模型。第一阶段分类器F1=0.91，第二阶段回归模型RMSE=1.00天，R^2=0.76。

Conclusion: 该框架为临床医生提供了严重加重前的早期预警和可操作的提前时间，有望改善AECOPD的家庭监测和早期干预。

Abstract: Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) can worsen rapidly, making timely prediction a clinical priority. Most existing machine learning approaches rely on episodically collected clinical variables, introducing delays that limit their practical utility in home monitoring settings. Home ventilators offer a lower-latency alternative, producing a near-continuous record of respiratory status during daily use. However existing ventilator-based approaches either compress the waveform into handcrafted features or focus primarily on binary risk classification, leaving the timing of an impending event unresolved. In this paper, we present a two-stage framework that operates directly on raw pressure and flow waveforms from the most recent seven days of home ventilator use. The first-stage classification model identifies patients at high risk of a severe exacerbation. The second-stage regression model then estimates how many days remain before the event occurs. Our experimental results demonstrate that the two-stage model outperforms traditional baseline models on both risk classification and time-to-event estimation, with our selected Stage 1 classifier achieving F1 = 0.91 and our Stage 2 regression model achieving RMSE = 1.00 days and R^2 = 0.76, giving clinicians both an early warning and actionable lead time before a severe exacerbation occurs.

</details>


### [102] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息流形和复几何角度研究复参数网络的损失景观，发现Calabi-Yau信息流形中的病态曲率条件会破坏优化保证，特别是负曲率（包括截面曲率和负定Ricci曲率）会导致损失景观劣化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解复参数网络（如复值神经网络）的损失景观几何特性，特别是从信息论流形视角和经典优化保证出发，探究复几何结构如何影响优化过程和理论保证。

Method: 采用Kähler信息度量和Wirtinger Hessian构建对数似然势，使用自然梯度下降保持全纯切丛中的下降路径，重点研究Calabi-Yau信息流形及固定行列式条件下的低秩度量影响。

Result: 发现Calabi-Yau度量下的病态曲率条件（特别是通过低秩度量导致的爆破效应）会破坏理论保证；负截面曲率和负定Ricci曲率会颠覆损失景观，导致神经网络保证失效。

Conclusion: 复参数网络的损失景观受到深层几何结构的影响，负曲率现象为理解神经网络初始化渐近行为和失效模式提供了新的几何分析视角。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition. Under a fixed determinant, a metric almost low rank up to an eigenvalue tolerance implies a blow-up effect. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist in a geometric analytic modality, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [103] [Unregularized Convergence of Single-Loop, Entropy-Regularized Natural Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19587)
*Zhiqiang Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单循环熵正则化自然Actor-Critic算法，通过固定温度调节和最小动作间隙假设，在无正则化目标上实现了比现有方法更快的收敛速率，突破了传统统计障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵正则化在实践中广泛用于稳定和加速自然策略梯度方法，但其能否为无正则化目标提供更快收敛速率的理论保证尚不充分。现有理论分析多依赖双循环架构和线性熵惩罚，与实践中的单循环方法存在差距，需要填补理论与实践的鸿沟。

Method: 提出单循环熵正则化自然Actor-Critic算法，采用兼容线性函数逼近和无中心化评论家（uncentered critic）以在Fisher信息矩阵退化时保持稳定。将优化分为随机和确定性两种机制：随机机制通过联合Lyapunov递归融合Actor-Critic更新；确定性机制转向策略镜像下降框架。引入指数转换机制，利用无正则化MDP中的正最小动作间隙，建立正则化与无正则化性能间隙的映射关系。

Result: 通过固定温度调节，算法在无正则化目标上实现加速收敛：随机机制达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-1})$收敛率；确定性机制的平均迭代达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-2/3})$，最后迭代达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-1/3})$。在表格设置中，正动作间隙分析产生$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-2/3})$平均迭代收敛率，突破了无正动作间隙时的$\mathcal{O}(T_{total}^{-1/2})$最坏情况统计障碍。

Conclusion: 本文证明了适当调参的单循环熵正则化方法可以显著加速无正则化目标的收敛，填补了理论与实践的鸿沟。通过最小动作间隙假设和指数转换机制，在理论上获得了优于传统方法的收敛保证，为强化学习算法设计提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: While entropy regularization is widely used to stabilize and accelerate Natural Policy Gradient methods, its ability to yield faster convergence rates for the unregularized objective remains underexplored. Existing analyses often rely on double-loop architectures and invoke a linear entropy penalty. To bridge the gap between theory and practice, we analyze a single-loop, entropy-regularized Natural Actor-Critic algorithm under compatible linear function approximation. By training an uncentered critic, our critic tracking can remain stable even as the training policy approaches determinism and the Fisher information matrix degenerates. We focus on two primary regimes for the optimization landscape: a Stochastic Regime, where we fuse coupled actor-critic updates into a joint Lyapunov recurrence, and a Deterministic Regime, where we pivot to a Policy Mirror Descent framework to circumvent the collapse of Euclidean geometry. By exploiting a positive Minimal Action Gap in the unregularized Markov decision process, we introduce an Exponential Translation mechanism that maps the regularized gap to the unregularized one up to an exponentially decaying tail. By tuning the fixed temperature, our algorithm achieves accelerated unregularized convergence rates, up to approximation-error terms: $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-1})$ in the Stochastic Regime, and $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-2/3})$ for the average iterate alongside $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-1/3})$ for the last iterate in the Deterministic Regime. Here, $T_{total}$ denotes the total number of stochastic critic updates (or Monte Carlo rollouts). Furthermore, in the tabular setting, our positive-action-gap analysis yields a $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T_{total}^{-2/3})$ average-iterate rate, surpassing the $\mathcal{O}(T_{total}^{-1/2})$ worst-case statistical barrier that applies without a positive action margin.

</details>


### [104] [Complementary, Not Cumulative: Interaction Effects in Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Navier-Stokes Vortex Shedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19632)
*Devesh Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在DFG圆柱尾流基准测试中，单独使用各种PINN改进技术均无效，但SIREN激活函数与因果加权组合可达到4.1%的相对L2误差，进一步添加技术反而导致性能急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN作为一种替代CFD求解器的方法受到广泛关注，各种改进技术层出不穷，但这些技术通常单独验证，缺乏对其组合效果的系统研究，亟需探究这些技术是否能够协同工作。

Method: 以DFG/Schafer-Turek非定常圆柱尾流基准测试为研究对象，系统评估多种PINN训练改进技术的单独效果与组合效果，包括周期性激活函数（SIREN）、因果加权等方法，并以OpenFOAM参考解作为基准进行对比验证。

Result: 单独使用时，几乎所有技术都不优于未经处理的基准模型；SIREN激活函数与因果加权的组合突破了原有性能瓶颈，速度场和压力场的平均相对L2误差降至4.1%；继续添加其他技术会导致灾难性的性能退化，表明各种改进技术之间存在非线性交互作用。

Conclusion: PINN训练改进技术之间存在复杂的非线性交互关系，并非所有有效技术都能协同工作；简单的SIREN激活函数与因果加权组合效果最佳，更复杂的训练配方未必带来更好的性能；研究揭示了PINN技术组合的优化方向，强调了系统性验证技术交互的重要性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) embed governing partial differential equations directly into the training loss, offering a promising alternative to costly CFD solvers for unsteady flows. Yet the growing list of techniques proposed to improve PINN training is typically validated one at a time, leaving open whether these techniques actually compose. We study this question in depth on the DFG/Schafer-Turek unsteady cylinder wake benchmark. In isolation, nearly every technique performs no better than an untreated baseline. However, combining periodic (SIREN) activations with causal weighting unlocks a previously inaccessible regime, reconstructing velocity and pressure fields to within 4.1% average relative L2 error against an OpenFOAM reference solution. Adding further techniques instead causes catastrophic performance degradation, demonstrating that individually effective PINN interventions can interact nonlinearly and that more elaborate training recipes are not necessarily better.

</details>


### [105] [Time-Uniform Self-Normalized Concentration for Discounted Least Squares: Limits and Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19643)
*Yi-Shan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了广泛使用的加权自归一化浓度不等式的时间一致性保证在折扣最小二乘估计中是错误的，并提供了正确的修正版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的加权自归一化浓度不等式声称在非平稳问题中为折扣最小二乘估计器提供时间一致性保证，但这一声明缺乏严格的数学验证。由于这类不等式是bandit和强化学习分析中的标准工具，其正确性对后续理论分析至关重要。

Method: 通过构造一个简单的标量高斯反例证明原声称的错误；对固定折扣和正则化参数，证明任意确定性anytime边界的下界；识别原始证明中的错误：不同终止时间使用不同高斯混合分布，无法形成超鞅；给出有效的有限和无限视界修正方案。

Result: 证明了原声称的有界半径在高斯反例中以概率1被突破；对于固定折扣和正则化参数，当δ≤1/2且T/δ足够大时，任何确定性anytime边界的下界至少为R√log(T/δ)；原始证明错误在于不同终止时间的高斯混合分布不构成超鞅；加权不等式在固定确定性时间仍然有效。

Conclusion: 原加权扩展的时间一致性保证是错误的，但固定时间版本仍有效。提供了正确的有限和无限视界修正，为下游分析提供了可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Self-normalized concentration inequalities are standard tools in bandit and reinforcement-learning analyses. A widely used weighted extension claims an analogous time-uniform guarantee for discounted least-squares estimators in non-stationary problems. A simple scalar Gaussian counterexample with a fixed parameter shows that the claimed bounded radius is crossed with probability one. For fixed discount and regularization parameters, we further show that, when $δ\leq1/2$ and $T/δ$ is sufficiently large, any deterministic anytime boundary valid uniformly over the stated conditionally sub-Gaussian model class must be at least of order $R\sqrt{\log(T/δ)}$ at some time by horizon $T$; for nondecreasing boundaries, this order is required at time $T$.
