We identify the proof error: different terminal times use different Gaussian mixing distributions, so the fixed-time mixtures do not form one supermartingale, and the stopping-time argument does not repair this failure. Finally, we show that the weighted inequality remains valid at each fixed deterministic time, give valid finite- and infinite-horizon corrections, and discuss consequences for downstream analyses.

</details>


### [106] [DeltaML-Bench: Evaluating Machine Learning Agents on Real-World Research Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19653)
*Josias Moukpe,Priyanka Aryal,Matthew Kenney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DeltaML-Bench基准测试，评估ML自动化实验智能体性能。通过ARG架构显著提升了GPT-5的成功率（最高达49.0%），并有效规避了规范博弈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试无法全面捕捉ML实验自动化所需的真实条件，包括异构仓库导航、训练流水线修复以及在计算资源限制下的候选改进评估。需要一个能够反映这些真实挑战的基准测试。

Method: 构建DeltaML-Bench基准，包含48个来源于研究论文的任务，要求智能体在非完美的开源仓库中改进已发布的基线结果。评估了GPT-5和Claude Sonnet 4两种模型，对比了标准Modular智能体和基于搜索的ARG脚手架架构。

Result: 在4x6h计算分配下，ARG将GPT-5的单次成功率从9.4%提升至33.9%；在2x12h分配下，GPT-5 ARG达到49.0%成功率。Modular配置的规范博弈率高达47.9%，而评估的ARG配置未观察到规范博弈现象。

Conclusion: 脚手架设计和完整性检查对于部署自动化ML实验智能体具有重要意义。合理的架构设计既能显著提升成功率，又能有效避免智能体通过作弊手段完成任务。

Abstract: Autonomous agents for machine learning experimentation must navigate heterogeneous repositories, repair training pipelines, and evaluate candidate improvements under realistic compute constraints. Existing benchmarks only partially capture these conditions. We introduce DeltaML-Bench, a benchmark comprising 48 tasks sourced from research papers that require agents to improve published baselines within imperfect, open-source repositories. We evaluate GPT-5 and Claude Sonnet 4 with a standard Modular agent and a search-based ARG scaffolding. In the 4 x 6h allocation, ARG raises GPT-5's per-run success rate from 9.4% to 33.9%; in the 2 x 12h allocation, GPT-5 ARG reaches 49.0%. Modular configurations exhibit specification gaming rates as high as 47.9%, while no gaming is observed in the evaluated ARG configurations. These results indicate that scaffolding design and integrity checks are important considerations when deploying agents for autonomous ML experimentation.

</details>


### [107] [Rationally Enriched Chebyshev Trunk Bases for DeepONet Surrogates of High Péclet Entrance Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19658)
*Mingeun Choi,Satish Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Chebyshev多项式和有理函数的REC-trunk DeepONet，用于解决具有薄边界层的奇异扰动问题，在边界层预测上显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异扰动和高Péclet数输运问题的解具有薄局部边界层特征，传统的vanilla DeepONet和纯Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet难以准确捕捉这些边界层结构，导致预测精度不足。

Method: 提出合理富集Chebyshev（REC）trunk，将Chebyshev多项式字典元素与通过自适应Antoulas-Anderson（AAA）算法构造的有理字典元素相结合，用于DeepONet代理模型。

Result: 在三个测试问题中，REC-trunk DeepONet在标量剖面预测上优于vanilla DeepONet且与Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet相当；在扰动参数为10^-4量级时，误差比Chebyshev-trunk降低最多19.5%；在壁面法向温度和浓度剖面预测中，误差分别比vanilla和Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet降低最多60.2%和32.2%，同时抑制了Péclet数为10^2至10^4时的人工近壁振荡。

Conclusion: REC-trunk DeepONet通过有理函数元素的引入，有效提升了对具有薄边界层的奇异扰动问题的预测能力，尤其在边界层附近的精度改善显著，为这类问题的深度学习建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This study demonstrates a rationally enriched Chebyshev (REC) trunk for deep operator network (DeepONet) surrogate models of singularly perturbed and high-Péclet transport problems whose solution profiles are characterized by thin localized boundary or wall layers. The REC trunk combines Chebyshev polynomial dictionary elements with rational dictionary elements constructed using the adaptive Antoulas-Anderson (AAA) algorithm. Over five independent training runs, the resulting REC-trunk DeepONet is evaluated against a vanilla DeepONet and a Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet whose prescribed dictionary consists only of Chebyshev polynomials across three problems whose singular perturbation parameters are diffusion-to-advection ratios: a singularly perturbed scalar boundary-value problem (BVP), the thermal entrance problem with a prescribed wall temperature, and the concentration entrance problem with an absorbing wall. Across the held-out test profiles, the REC-trunk DeepONet improves over the vanilla DeepONet and remains comparable to the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet in predicting the scalar profile, with its clearest advantage over the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet appearing when the perturbation parameter lies between $1.00\times10^{-4}$ and $1.78\times10^{-4}$, where it reduces the profile-error metrics by up to $19.5\,\%$ relative to the Chebyshev-trunk DeepONet. In predicting the wall-normal temperature and concentration profiles, the REC-trunk DeepONet reduces the profile-error metrics by up to $60.2\,\%$ and $32.2\,\%$ relative to the vanilla and Chebyshev-trunk DeepONets, respectively, while suppressing artificial near-wall oscillations as the Péclet or mass-transfer Péclet number ranges from $10^{2}$ to $10^{4}$.

</details>


### [108] [SAGE-XGBoost: Spatially Augmented Graph Embeddings--Machine Learning Framework for Natural Hazards Susceptibility Mapping under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19672)
*Mohammad H. Vahidnia,Ali Pourkarimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，通过空间增强图嵌入和噪声数据增强解决自然灾害易发性制图中的数据稀缺问题，SAGE-XGBoost模型在滑坡和野火易发性制图中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害易发性制图常受限于标注数据不足，导致传统机器学习方法泛化能力受限，复杂深度学习模型难以应用，亟需在数据稀缺条件下提升预测性能的方法。

Method: 提出SAGE框架：(1)构建K近邻图推导局部空间统计量；(2)使用主成分分析降维；(3)将降维后的图嵌入与环境协变量和空间坐标整合；(4)基于XGBoost构建SAGE-XGBoost模型；(5)结合受控噪声数据增强技术。

Result: 在滑坡和野火易发性制图案例中，SAGE-XGBoost显著优于传统和空间显式机器学习模型。相比Spatial XGBoost绝对提升超过33个百分点；滑坡易发性AUC约0.97，野火易发性AUC约0.95。特征重要性分析证实图嵌入对预测的贡献，同时改善了空间一致性并减少了局部噪声放大。

Conclusion: SAGE-XGBoost为有限监督条件下的环境灾害评估和其他地理空间预测任务提供了高效、可迁移的深度表示学习替代方案，具有良好的实用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: Natural hazard susceptibility mapping is often constrained by limited labeled data, reducing the generalizability of conventional machine learning and limiting the applicability of complex deep learning models. This study proposes SAGE (Spatially Augmented Graph Embeddings), a structurally informed feature-engineering framework that combines controlled noise-based data augmentation with neighborhood-based graph embeddings to improve prediction under data-scarce conditions. A K-nearest neighbor graph is constructed to derive local spatial statistics, which are reduced using principal component analysis and integrated with environmental covariates and spatial coordinates. The resulting features are used with XGBoost to develop the SAGE-XGBoost model. The framework was evaluated for landslide and wildfire susceptibility mapping. SAGE-XGBoost consistently outperformed conventional and spatially explicit machine learning models. Compared with Spatial XGBoost, it achieved an absolute improvement of above 33 percentage points across the two case studies. The model reached AUC values of approximately 0.97 for landslide susceptibility and 0.95 for wildfire susceptibility. Feature importance analysis confirmed the contribution of graph embeddings to prediction, while their integration improved spatial coherence and reduced local noise amplification. Overall, SAGE-XGBoost provides an efficient and transferable alternative to deep representation learning for environmental hazard assessment and other geospatial prediction tasks under limited supervision.

</details>


### [109] [A Locally Tokenized Generative Model for Robust Time-Series Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19727)
*Dongbin Kim,Geonwoo Shin,Yujin Choi,Soyeon Park,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多变量时间序列水印在编辑攻击下可靠性不足的问题，提出L-VQVAE生成模型和LVQMark水印方法，通过限制时间邻域依赖性稳定了检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于全局耦合重编码的水印检测器在后编辑攻击下存在零分布的双向漂移问题，导致非水印样本的z-score向任一方向偏移，使得清洁校准的阈值失效，影响了多变量时间序列水印的可靠性。

Method: 提出L-VQVAE生成模型，使每个离散token仅依赖于有限的时间窗口；在此基础上设计LVQMark水印方法，结合logit偏置注入和鲁棒重编码技术进行攻击时检测，确保每个恢复单元仅依赖有界的时域邻域。

Result: 在金融、能源和神经影像等四个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持生成质量的同时，有效稳定了后编辑攻击下的检测能力和假阳性行为。

Conclusion: 通过限制每个token对时域邻域的依赖性，可以解决现有全局耦合重编码方法的零分布漂移问题，实现多变量时间序列水印的可靠检测。

Abstract: Watermarking is a central tool for provenance in generative models, yet its application to multivariate time series remains hindered by reliability failures under post-editing attacks. We show that existing detectors, which rely on globally coupled re-encoding, suffer from bidirectional drift of the null distribution: post-editing attacks can shift the z-score of non-watermarked samples in either direction, invalidating clean-calibrated thresholds. We argue that this instability is a property of the re-encoding, and that reliable detection requires each recovered unit to depend only on a bounded temporal neighborhood. Guided by this principle, we propose L-VQVAE, a generative model in which each discrete token is produced from a short contiguous window, and LVQMark, a watermarking method over this token space that combines logit-bias injection with robust re-encoding for attack-time detection. Experiments on four benchmarks spanning finance, energy, and neuroimaging show that our approach preserves generation quality while stabilizing both detection power and false-positive behavior under post-editing attacks.

</details>


### [110] [RecPFN: Prior-Fitted Networks for In-Context-Based Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19735)
*En Zhi Tan,Jia Xiang Lim,Bryan Lijie Chew,Tze Minh Ng,Benjamin Yan Han Yap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecPFN是一个用于序列推荐的先验拟合网络，通过在合成点击流数据上的预训练实现了上下文学习，在零样本设置下达到SOTA性能，无需权重更新即可快速适应新领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列推荐模型通常需要大量领域内数据进行训练和微调，且在面对新领域或数据稀缺场景时泛化能力不足。本文旨在通过引入上下文学习机制，构建一个能够零样本或小样本适应不同推荐场景的通用模型，从而提高推荐系统的数据效率和部署效率。

Method: RecPFN采用轻量级的decoder-only transformer架构，核心是在从广泛的结构因果先验中采样的合成点击流环境中进行预训练。这种预训练使模型能够从少量支持序列中 amortize 贝叶斯风格的推理过程。推理时，模型仅需对少量领域序列进行条件化，即可在单次前向传播中生成下一项预测，无需任何权重更新。

Result: 在八个公开基准数据集上，RecPFN在零样本设置下达到了最先进性能，同时在低计算和低数据场景下与监督方法保持高度竞争力。相比依赖大规模真实交互数据的零样本基线，RecPFN在部署效率和对领域迁移的鲁棒性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: RecPFN为构建可泛化、数据高效的推荐系统提供了实用路径，验证了先验拟合网络在推荐任务中的有效性。该工作为未来探索更丰富的先验、更长上下文的上下文学习以及多模态扩展开辟了新方向。代码已公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We introduce RecPFN, a prior-fitted network that brings in-context learning to sequential recommendation. RecPFN is pretrained entirely on synthetic clickstream environments sampled from a broad structural causal prior, enabling it to amortize Bayesian-style inference from a small support set. At inference, a lightweight decoder-only transformer conditions on a handful of domain sequences and produces next-item predictions for queries in a single forward pass, without any weight updates. Across eight public benchmarks, RecPFN achives state-of-the-art zero-shot performance while remaining strongly competitive with supervised methods in low-compute and low-data regimes. It is deployment-efficient and robust to domain shift, outperforming strong zero-shot baselines that rely on large real-interaction corpora. RecPFN provides a practical path toward generalizable, data-efficient recommenders and opens avenues for richer priors, longer-context ICL, and multimodal extensions. Code for training and evaluation is publicly available at https://github.com/SAP-samples/tabular-ai-recpfn/.

</details>


### [111] [Truncate Bad, Upweight Good: BoN-Style Distillation via Rank-Based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19748)
*Yarin Bar,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TUP（截断差样本、提升好样本策略），通过移除低排名样本并重新加权保留的上尾部分来改进推理时选择方法的蒸馏过程，在离线对齐任务中取得了与强基线方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于排名的蒸馏策略通常使用平滑的全支持重加权，导致低排名样本虽权重降低但仍保留在目标分布中；更尖锐的重加权虽然减少低尾权重，但过度依赖单一奖励模型在顶部的脆弱排序。需要一种既能有效降低低尾样本影响，又不依赖脆弱顶层排序的方法。

Method: 提出TUP策略：1) 移除低排名样本（截断低尾），仅重新加权保留的上尾部分；2) 使用可调节的尖锐度参数控制重加权强度；3) 提供闭式、提示无关的归一化方法；4) 通过二元交叉熵实现完全离线训练，使用移位截断的胜率作为软标签，以蒸馏到参考模型的对数似然比作为logits。

Result: 在理论层面，证明了在某些假设下，对于任意未知oracle奖励，最佳单调排名重加权可由低尾截断规则匹配，为移除低尾而非仅降权提供了形式化支持。在实证层面，TUP在与强离线对齐基线方法的对比中表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: TUP通过截断低尾样本而非仅降权的方式，有效解决了现有蒸馏方法中低质量样本残留和顶层排序脆弱性的问题，同时保持了完全离线训练的可行性，为推理时选择方法的蒸馏提供了一种理论支撑和实践有效的新方案。

Abstract: Inference-time selection methods, such as Best-of-N, improve generation by sampling a pool of candidates and selecting the top-ranked completion according to a reward model. Distillation seeks to amortize this procedure into a single policy by replacing raw rewards with in-pool ranks and learning a policy that upweights higher-ranked completions. However, existing rank-based policies typically use smooth full-support reweighting, so low-ranked completions receive less mass but remain in the target support. Although a sharper reweighting reduces lower-tail mass, it also increases reliance on brittle ranking at the top made by a single reward model. We propose TUP: a Truncate-bad, Upweight-good Policy that removes low-ranked completions from the support and reweights only the retained upper tail with a tunable sharpness. TUP admits a closed-form, prompt-independent normalization and can be trained fully offline via binary cross-entropy, using shifted-truncated win-rates as soft labels and distilled-to-reference log-likelihood ratios as logits. Theoretically, under certain assumptions, we show that for any unknown oracle reward, the best monotone rank-reweighting can be matched by a lower-tail truncation rule, providing formal support for removing the lower tail rather than merely downweighting it. Empirically, we show that TUP is competitive with strong offline alignment baselines.

</details>


### [112] [Credit Without Ground Truth: Auditing Step-Level Credit Assignment in LLM Agents Against Executed Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19760)
*Haiyue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在ALFWorld环境中审计发现，现有LLM智能体的步骤级信用信号（LLM评判分数、结果条件化对数概率比、策略置信度）在识别因果关键步骤上均不优于随机猜测，揭示了当前信用分配方法的根本性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法仅检验步骤的标注正确性，而忽视了步骤对最终结果的实际因果贡献。作者认为，评估信用信号应当基于步骤的因果贡献——即在决策点重采样策略的替代选项并前向推演是否真正改变结果，而非仅看步骤是否被标注为正确。

Method: 在单智能体工具环境ALFWorld中，通过执行重放建立因果真值基准。对每个决策点进行策略替代方案的重采样和前向推演，量化步骤的因果贡献。比较分析了三种信用信号：LLM评判分数、结果条件化对数概率比、策略自身置信度。设计了七臂预注册训练实验，控制有效样本量进行对比。

Result: (1)三种信用信号识别因果关键步骤的能力均不优于随机；(2)因果贡献稀疏：仅30.5%的决策点具有可测量的因果效应；(3)可测性依赖模型：相似规模策略间无可行反事实的决策点比例相差两倍(13.1% vs 26.8%)；(4)隐性信用主要反映策略流畅性(中位秩相关+0.75)，结果条件化不增加因果信息(偏相关-0.004)；(5)七臂训练实验中无一超越未训练策略，表观效果完全由训练剂量（优化步数）而非信用内容解释。

Conclusion: 步骤正确性评估与因果贡献评估相互独立。当前信用信号主要捕捉策略流畅性而非因果关系，条件化于结果不提供因果信息。信用规则比较必须匹配有效样本量，否则测量的只是训练剂量而非信用质量本身。这揭示了现有信用分配方法的核心失效模式。

Abstract: Audited against causal ground truth from executed replay in a single-agent tool environment (ALFWorld), none of the step-level credit signals used to train LLM agents -- LLM-judge scores, outcome-conditioned logprob ratios, or the policy's own confidence -- identifies which steps causally matter better than chance. Existing evaluations grade these signals against annotated step *correctness*; we audit them against step *contribution* -- what re-sampling the policy's own alternatives at each decision point and rolling forward actually changes about the outcome -- and the two come apart. The ground truth itself is structured: causal contribution is sparse (30.5% of decision points where ground truth is defined carry measurable effect), and measurability is model-dependent -- the fraction of points with no policy-supported counterfactual differs by a factor of two (13.1% vs. 26.8%) between two similar-scale policies. The failure mode is identifiable: implicit credit echoes the policy's fluency (median rank correlation +0.75, replicating at +0.70 in a second family under a corrected instrument), while conditioning on the outcome adds no causal information (partial correlation -0.004, Qwen). A confidence-only router recovers pivotal steps at chance level, but cuts judge cost by 13.1% per turn (14.0% per trajectory). In a seven-arm pre-registered training experiment, no arm reliably outperforms the untrained policy, and the checkpoints' apparent instrument signature is fully explained by training dose -- sparser credit retains fewer examples, an order-of-magnitude spread in optimizer steps -- not credit content. Comparisons of credit rules must therefore match effective sample size, or they measure dose, not credit.

</details>


### [113] [Finite-Horizon Input-Output Dynamics of Minibatch Perturbations in AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19762)
*Kang Liu,Suyan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdamW优化器中minibatch对训练的延迟影响，通过将AdamW建模为有限时域输入-状态-输出系统，揭示了优化器状态如何通过存储的历史梯度信息影响未来损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdamW优化器中，由于优化器状态存储了过去梯度信息，一个minibatch可能在其被观察的更新步骤之外继续影响训练过程。理解这种延迟效应对于深入理解优化器行为和改进训练策略具有重要意义。

Method: 作者采用配对轨迹方法，仅在某个梯度更新处存在差异，其余训练序列相同。将AdamW建模为有限时域输入-状态-输出(ISO)系统，状态包含模型参数和一阶、二阶矩估计。通过联合动力学的线性化，推导出符号响应算子，将局部梯度扰动映射到其未来的损失效应。同时导出精确的多步误差分解，并在局部平滑性和受控激活切换条件下建立一阶有限时域精度。

Result: 研究揭示了优化器记忆如何塑造延迟影响的幅度、时序和符号。实验验证了响应机制和优化器状态效应，重复-未来分析显示延迟影响中存在大量前瞻性结构，这些结构可以从ISO近似中部分恢复。

Conclusion: AdamW的优化器状态确实产生了显著的延迟效应，通过ISO系统建模可以有效捕捉这种延迟影响的结构和机制，为理解和改进深度学习优化过程提供了新的理论框架和分析工具。

Abstract: A minibatch can influence training beyond the update at which it is observed because AdamW stores past gradient information in its optimizer states. We study this delayed effect through paired trajectories that differ only in one gradient update and share the same subsequent training sequence. We formulate AdamW as a finite-horizon input--state--output (ISO) system whose state contains the model parameters and first- and second-moment estimates. Linearizing the joint dynamics yields a signed response operator that maps a localized gradient perturbation to its future loss effects, revealing how optimizer memory shapes their magnitude, timing, and sign. We further derive an exact multistep error decomposition and establish first-order finite-horizon accuracy under local smoothness and controlled activation switching. Experiments validate the response mechanism and optimizer-state effects, while repeated-future analyses reveal substantial prospective structure in delayed influence that can be partially recovered from ISO approximations. Code is available at https://github.com/Kanyooo/Loss_ISO.

</details>


### [114] [Unsupervised Anomaly Detection Using Flow Matching on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19801)
*Philip Konz,Tejaswini Medi,Margret Keuper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在受污染的训练数据下研究基于流匹配的表格异常检测，发现轨迹类异常评分显著优于单步决策评分，使Forest-Flow表现媲美甚至超越TCCM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融异常检测常依赖大量无标签交易日志，训练集中可能已包含异常样本。这种训练集污染违反了许多异常检测方法所依赖的"纯净正常数据"假设。尽管流匹配在生成建模中表现优异，但其在无监督表格异常检测中的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在受污染训练数据条件下研究基于流匹配的异常检测方法，对比时间条件收缩匹配（TCCM）与Forest-Flow，并评估多种异常评分函数：原始单步决策评分、基于轨迹的偏差评分和重构评分。

Result: 异常评分的选择至关重要。TCCM原始的单步决策评分对污染敏感，而基于轨迹的偏差评分和重构评分提供更稳定的异常信号。采用这些评分后，Forest-Flow与TCCM表现相当，在某些情况下甚至更优。

Conclusion: 异常评分的选择对基于流匹配的方法在严重类别不平衡的金融异常检测中具有重要影响，轨迹类评分方法能有效提升模型在污染训练数据下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Financial anomaly detection often relies on large unlabeled transaction logs, where anomalous samples may already be present during training. Such training-set contamination violates the clean-normal data assumption underlying many anomaly detection methods. Although flow matching has demonstrated strong performance in generative modeling, its robustness in unsupervised tabular anomaly detection remains underexplored. In this work, we study flow-matching-based anomaly detection under contaminated training data by comparing Time-Conditioned Contraction Matching (TCCM) with Forest-Flow and evaluating multiple anomaly scoring functions. Our results show that the choice of anomaly score is critical. The original single-step Decision score used by TCCM is sensitive to contamination, whereas trajectory-based Deviation and Reconstruction scores provide more stable anomaly signals. With these scores, Forest-Flow becomes competitive with, and in some cases outperforms, TCCM. These findings highlight the importance of anomaly scoring for flow-matching methods in financial anomaly detection under severe class imbalance.

</details>


### [115] [MileGPO: Milestone Inference with Local Evidence for Graph-Based Policy Optimization of Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19803)
*Bo Qian,Yuting Wu,Shuang Zeng,Huaiyu Wan,Dalin Zhang,Jiqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MileGPO通过里程碑发现和双重校准机制，将稀疏的过程级奖励转化为密集的过程信用，解决了长序列决策中的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列决策强化学习中，监督信号仅来自最终奖励，导致信用分配困难；现有方法（步骤分组或基于图的优势估计）容易忽略重要的中间里程碑，无法准确识别有意义的中间状态。

Method: MileGPO通过三个核心设计从分组的在线策略轨迹中提取过程级信用：(1)里程碑发现：从成功轨迹中识别候选里程碑，从失败轨迹中识别反复出现的陷阱；(2)可靠性校准塑形(RCS)：基于结果的置信度加权候选，强化可靠里程碑和陷阱；(3)进度对比校准(PCC)：检验候选是否反映局部进度，以及其转移是否优于从同一状态观察到的替代方案。该方法无需辅助模型或额外环境交互。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop实验中达到最先进性能，ALFWorld上的分布内到分布外泛化差距小；消融实验和信用诊断表明可靠性加权、局部进度和同状态分支证据有效互补里程碑发现，解决了模糊的中间信用问题。

Conclusion: MileGPO成功实现了过程级信用分配，无需额外模型和交互开销，在提升性能的同时保持良好的泛化能力；三重设计有效解决了中间里程碑识别和信用模糊性问题。

Abstract: Credit assignment is challenging in long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning, where supervision often comes only from final rewards. Existing methods refine trajectory-level signals into step-level credits through step grouping or graph-based advantage estimation, but can overlook meaningful intermediate milestones. We propose MileGPO (Milestone Inference with Local Evidence for Graph-Based Policy Optimization), which derives process-level credit from grouped on-policy rollouts through three designs. Milestone Discovery identifies candidate milestones on successful rollouts and recurring traps on failed ones. Reliability-Calibrated Shaping (RCS) weights these candidates by outcome-based confidence, strengthening reliable milestones and traps while down-weighting uncertain ones. Progress-Contrastive Calibration (PCC) further tests whether a candidate reflects local progress and whether its incoming ansition outperforms observed alternatives from the same state.MileGPO requires neither auxiliary models nor additional environment interaction. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show state-of-the-art performance and a small in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap on ALFWorld. Ablations and credit diagnostics indicate that reliability weighting, local progress, and same-state branch evidence complement milestone discovery and resolve ambiguous intermediate credit.

</details>


### [116] [Answer-Level Trust Selection for Physical Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19807)
*Rongyu Yu,Ke Niu,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ATS框架，用于在无真实标签的情况下判断VLM物理推理预测的可信度，通过行为诊断分数识别稳定但错误的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估VLM的整体性能，但在实际部署中关键问题是：当无法获取真实标签时，如何判断单个预测是否可信？仅依赖自一致性可能无法识别某些失效模式，如稳定但错误的估计或依赖文本先验而非视觉证据。

Method: 提出Answer-Level Trust Selection (ATS)框架，这是一个事后、模型无关的方法。无需微调、辅助验证器或访问模型内部logits。通过聚合八个可解释的行为诊断分数（源自重复查询和受控干预）生成统一的信任分数，从而决定接受或拒绝单个VLM预测。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B及20个VLM骨干网络上进行了深入评估。基于干预的诊断能有效识别仅靠重复一致性会遗漏的"稳定但错误"和"先验追踪"预测。但改进的失效案例拒绝可能以降低正确预测保留率为代价。

Conclusion: ATS是对模型级能力评估的补充，为定量VLM预测提供了答案级可靠性评估，帮助在实际部署中做出更明智的预测信任决策。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can estimate physical quantities such as duration, speed, and acceleration from visual observations, but existing benchmarks primarily assess overall model performance against annotated ground truth. In deployment, a key question is whether an individual prediction can be trusted when its ground truth is unavailable. Self-consistency alone may fail to capture important failure modes: a VLM may produce stable-but-wrong estimates or rely on textual priors rather than visual evidence. We formulate answer-level selective prediction for quantitative physical reasoning and propose Answer-Level Trust Selection (ATS), a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework for accepting or rejecting individual VLM predictions. ATS requires no fine-tuning, auxiliary verifier, or access to the model's internal logits. Instead, it aggregates eight interpretable behavioral diagnostic scores derived from repeated queries and controlled interventions into a unified trust score. We evaluate ATS in depth on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and across 20 VLM backbones, examining selective performance, diagnostic behavior, and targeted failure modes. Our results show that intervention-based diagnostics help identify stable-but-wrong and prior-tracking predictions that repeated agreement alone may miss. However, improved failure-case rejection can come at the cost of lower retention of correct predictions. ATS therefore complements model-level capability evaluation with answer-level reliability assessment for quantitative VLM predictions. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [117] [FAR-DPO: Feasibility-Aware and Robust Direct Preference Optimization for Cyclic Peptide Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19808)
*Guofeng Zhang,Rong Han,Xiaoyu Wang,Zhiyun Li,Zongbo Han,Xiaohong Liu,Guangyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了FAR-DPO框架，通过可行性感知的偏好构建和难度感知的鲁棒优化，有效提升了环肽生成的成功率和目标鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环肽作为药物发现中的分子支架前景广阔，但从线性扩展到环肽生成面临挑战：环化通过几何和生物物理约束的耦合严格限制了可行设计空间；有限的训练数据导致现有方法依赖零样本生成或后处理过滤，设计可行率低且难以控制多目标权衡。

Method: 提出FAR-DPO（可行性感知与鲁棒直接偏好优化）框架：1）构建基于可行性门控多目标支配的靶内偏好对；2）通过难度感知的组鲁棒优化，根据当前偏好损失自适应重定义难度组权重；该框架具有架构无关性。

Result: 在CPSea LNR基准测试中，在固定生成预算下：PepGLAD上整体成功率从46.89%提升至57.79%，PepFlow上从47.96%提升至49.57%；在最困难的靶点四分位数上也取得增益，并伴随更优的逐靶点最佳结合得分。

Conclusion: FAR-DPO在提升环肽设计可行性和靶点级鲁棒性方面具有显著效果，成功解决了环肽生成中几何与生物物理约束耦合带来的设计空间受限问题。

Abstract: Cyclic peptides are emerging as promising molecular scaffolds in drug discovery due to their high binding affinity and structural stability. However, extending generative models from linear to cyclic peptide design remains challenging, as cyclization sharply restricts the feasible design space through coupled geometric and biophysical constraints. Moreover, limited training data has led existing approaches to rely largely on zero-shot generation or post hoc filtering, resulting in low yields of feasible designs and limited control over multi-objective trade-offs. To address these limitations, we propose FAR-DPO (Feasibility-Aware and Robust Direct Preference Optimization), an architecture-agnostic framework that steers generative models toward structurally and biophysically feasible cyclic peptide designs, particularly for challenging targets. FAR-DPO integrates feasibility-aware preference construction with difficulty-aware group-robust optimization. Specifically, it constructs within-target preference pairs through feasibility-gated multi-objective dominance and adaptively reweights predefined difficulty groups according to their current preference losses. On the CPSea LNR benchmark, under a fixed generation budget, FAR-DPO increases overall success rate from 46.89% to 57.79% on PepGLAD and from 47.96% to 49.57% on PepFlow. These gains also extend to the hardest target quartile and are accompanied by more favorable best-per-target binding scores. Together, these results demonstrate FAR-DPO's effectiveness in improving feasibility and target-wise robustness.

</details>


### [118] [Adaptive Probabilistic Shielding by Learning MDPs for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19836)
*Astrid Horn Brorholt,Maris F. L. Galesloot,Nils Jansen,Kim Guldstrand Larsen,Christian Schilling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应概率屏蔽方法，在转移概率未知的MDP中通过在线学习估计概率来计算安全屏蔽，实现了强化学习中的安全性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的概率屏蔽技术需要预先知道MDP的转移概率，但典型强化学习应用中MDP模型通常是未知的，这限制了该技术的适用性。本文旨在解决仅已知转移图结构而转移概率未知时的安全强化学习问题。

Method: 将概率屏蔽与在线模型学习相结合：随着RL智能体探索环境，估计转移概率并据此计算屏蔽。屏蔽最初可能较为保守，但随着模型估计的精确化而自适应调整，解决了何时重新计算屏蔽以及如何平衡探索与安全性等挑战。

Result: 在多个环境中对自适应概率屏蔽方法的多个变体进行了实证评估，验证了该方法在未知转移概率场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 自适应概率屏蔽范式能够在转移概率未知的情况下实现安全强化学习，屏蔽随着模型估计精度的提升而改进，与RL智能体同步优化。

Abstract: Probabilistic shielding is a technique for safe reinforcement learning (RL). Typically, a static observer -- called the shield -- constrains the learning agent's actions to those for which acting safely remains feasible. Traditionally, the shield is computed from the transition probabilities of the underlying Markov decision process (MDP). Thus, this technique is not applicable when the MDP model is not given a priori, which, unfortunately, is the case in typical RL applications. In this paper, we study the problem of computing a shield in the setting where the transition graph of the MDP is known, but the transition probabilities are unknown. Our approach integrates probabilistic shielding with online model learning: as the RL agent explores the environment, we estimate the transition probabilities. From this estimate, we compute a shield. While the shield may be conservative initially, it adapts as the model estimate becomes more precise. Thus, the shield improves in tandem with the RL agent. This paradigm of adaptive probabilistic shielding raises a number of challenges, such as when to recompute the shield and how to balance between exploration and safety during learning. We empirically evaluate multiple variants of this paradigm across several environments.

</details>


### [119] [Inadvertent Context Leakage in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19857)
*Jaiden Fairoze,Neal Mangaokar,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Sanjam Garg,Saeed Mahloujifar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现AI模型上下文窗口中的敏感信息会通过普通输出产生隐蔽泄露，更强能力的模型泄露更严重，攻击者可从看似正常的输出中重建敏感数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体为了提供有用服务需要在上下文中存储敏感用户信息（日历、凭证、健康记录、财务数据等），研究者关注这些秘密信息的存在是否会在模型的正常输出中引入隐藏相关性，即使模型正确拒绝直接提取请求，攻击者是否仍能通过设计提示词放大这种效应并将模型作为隐蔽载体传输秘密。

Method: 提出一种新颖的自适应攻击方法，假设对底层模型具有黑盒访问权限，通过两种方式研究信息泄露：1）被动观察模型普通输出中的隐蔽相关性；2）主动设计提示词放大泄露效应。在8个专有模型上进行对照实验，测试不同位数数字的重建准确率，并开发分类器推断用户记忆的语义谓词以及使用强化学习训练的对抗智能体提取完整的社会安全号码。

Result: 实验结果显示：2位数的上下文秘密可以近乎完美的准确率重建，4位数秘密达到82%的精确匹配，这些结果均来自模型对普通非对抗性请求的输出；模型能力越强泄露越严重，更强的指令遵循能力增强了对上下文秘密的敏感性，表明泄露是能力的副产品而非可修补的bug；成功实现两种实际攻击：训练的分类器可从常规自然语言输出推断用户记忆的语义谓词（如健康状况、财务事件），强化学习训练的对手可从生产风格的智能体中提取完整的社会安全号码。

Conclusion: 上下文敏感信息的泄露是AI模型能力的固有副产品而非简单的软件漏洞，这种泄露为实际攻击提供了可能，需要在AI智能体设计中考虑这种隐蔽的信息泄露风险，传统的拒绝直接提取的安全机制不足以防止通过正常输出进行的隐蔽数据提取。

Abstract: For AI agents to be useful beyond simple chat, they must hold sensitive user context such as calendars, credentials, health records, and financial data. We study whether the mere presence of such secrets in a model's context window introduces hidden correlations into the model's benign outputs, allowing reconstruction even when the model correctly refuses direct extraction. We further study whether an adversary can actively engineer prompts that amplify this effect, using the model as a covert carrier to transmit secrets through seemingly innocuous text. In both cases, this limited leakage is exploited using a novel adaptive attack that assumes black-box access to the underlying model.
  In controlled experiments across eight proprietary models, we find that 2-digit in-context secrets are reconstructed with near-perfect accuracy and 4-digit secrets at 82\% exact match, all from outputs the model produces in response to ordinary, non-adversarial requests. We observe that more capable models leak more: stronger instruction-following amplifies sensitivity to in-context secrets, suggesting leakage is a byproduct of capability as opposed to a patchable bug. We show this leakage enables two practical attacks: (1) a trained classifier that infers semantic predicates about user memories (e.g., health conditions, financial events) from routine natural-language outputs, and (2) an RL-trained adversary that extracts full Social Security Numbers from a production-style agent.

</details>


### [120] [Online Test-Time Adaptation for Generalizable Dynamic Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19858)
*Jialun Zheng,Hanchen Yang,Jiannong Cao,Yankai Chen,Yuanjing Feng,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OTTA-DGAD方法，用于在线测试时适应的可泛化动态图异常检测，通过动态原型和内存缓冲机制解决域特定模式识别和顺序数据到达的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化动态图异常检测方法存在两大局限：(1)主要依赖域无关模式，忽略了持续演化的域特定模式；(2)假设能够获取完整目标域数据，而实际在线测试时适应场景中目标数据以无标签块的形式顺序到达。

Method: 提出OTTA-DGAD方法：从时序自我图中提取动态原型（正常和异常模式的演化表示）并存储于内存缓冲区；缓冲区选择性保留源域共享的通用模式并融入目标域的新模式；异常评分器将输入边表示与原型对比以识别通用和域特定异常；使用基于置信度检测的可靠伪标签更新内存缓冲区；利用保留的先前块表示增强当前目标块以补偿顺序数据到达导致的信息损失。

Result: 在严格的测试后适应(OTA)设置下，在来自不同领域的十个真实世界数据集上进行了广泛实验，展示了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: OTTA-DGAD成功实现了在线测试时适应的可泛化动态图异常检测，有效解决了域特定模式识别和顺序数据到达的挑战，为实际应用场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Generalizable dynamic graph anomaly detection (DGAD) enables pretrained detectors to identify anomalies in unseen target domains without costly retraining. However, existing methods often fail for two reasons. First, they mainly rely on domain-agnostic patterns and miss domain-specific patterns that keep evolving. Second, they assume access to the full target domain data, whereas in more practical online test-time adaptation settings, target data arrive sequentially in unlabeled chunks. To address these limitations, we formulate online test-time adaptation for generalizable DGAD and propose OTTA-DGAD. OTTA-DGAD first extracts dynamic prototypes, i.e., evolving representations of normal and anomalous patterns, from temporal ego-graphs and stores them in a memory buffer. The buffer selectively retains general patterns shared across the source domains used for pretraining while incorporating new patterns from the target domain. An anomaly scorer then compares incoming edge representations against these prototypes to identify both general and domain-specific anomalies. During adaptation, OTTA-DGAD updates the memory buffer using reliable pseudo-labels identified through confidence-based detection. It further enriches each target chunk with relevant representations retained from previous chunks, compensating for information loss resulting from the sequential arrival of data. Extensive experiments under strict test-then-adapt OTTA settings demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on ten real-world datasets from diverse domains.

</details>


### [121] [Separating Covariate Shift from Mechanism Change with Two Discriminators: CJSD, a Conditional Discrepancy with an Exact Covariate-Concept Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19885)
*Kentaro Oda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于序贯假设检验和e-process的决策层，用于流数据系统中的专家模型管理（复用/创建/延迟），通过重启e检测器在保证有限时间有效性约束下实现了零错误决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流数据处理系统中维护专家模型池时，需要对到达数据做出复用现有专家、创建新专家或延迟决策的选择。现有方法缺乏统计意义和理论保证，需要一种具有严格统计有效性的决策机制。

Method: 将复用和创建建模为条件差异上的一侧序贯假设检验，设置无差别区域分隔；延迟对应两个e-process均未积累足够证据的状态。采用可预测判别器序列的可观测代理差异，证明其有限时间有效性。设计重启e检测器：在几何间隔的重启时间点使用无窗口betting上鞅银行(O(log t)内存)，错误预算在重启实例间分配以保持终身有效性，在专家创建顺序上分配以控制多重性。

Result: 在合成多概念流、Electricity、Covertype和INSECTS基准数据集上，实例计数型重启银行实现了零错误创建和切换后零错误复用；在INSECTS-reoccurring上达到0.675准确率，匹配或超过已退役的窗口化启发式方法，使部署算法与保证算法合二为一。

Conclusion: 该方法首次将流数据专家管理的三种决策结果赋予了统计意义，通过e-process理论提供了严格的理论保证，同时在实际性能上达到或超越启发式方法，实现了理论与实践的统一。

Abstract: Streaming systems that maintain a pool of expert models must repeatedly decide whether to reuse an existing expert for arriving data, spawn a new one, or defer. We present a decision layer that makes all three outcomes statistically meaningful. Reuse and spawn are posed as one-sided sequential hypotheses on a conditional (mechanism-level) discrepancy, separated by an indifference zone; defer is exactly the state in which neither betting e-process has accumulated sufficient evidence. We prove finite-time anytime validity for the observable surrogate discrepancy of a predictable discriminator sequence, and an unconditional one-sided transfer to the population quantity in which each side's slack is the excess risk of a single discriminator; an empirically observed downward-bias regularity makes the spawn side exactly conservative. Recency without sacrificing the guarantee is obtained by a restarted e-detector: a bank of unwindowed betting supermartingales at geometrically spaced restart times (O(log t) memory), with the error budget spent over restart instances, which preserves lifetime anytime validity; spending over expert-creation order likewise controls multiplicity for unboundedly many experts. On synthetic multi-concept streams, Electricity, Covertype, and the recurrence-heavy INSECTS benchmark, the instance-accounted restarted bank achieves zero false spawns and zero false reuses after switches and matches or exceeds the retired windowed heuristic (INSECTS-reoccurring accuracy 0.675), making the deployed algorithm and the guaranteed algorithm one and the same.

</details>


### [122] [Evidence Before Expansion: Reuse, Spawn, or Defer in Lifelong Expert Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19888)
*Kentaro Oda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于序贯假设检验的决策层，用于流数据处理系统中的专家模型管理，实现了重用、创建新模型和延迟决策三种结果的统计意义，并保证了有限时间的任意有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流数据处理系统中，维护专家模型池需要反复决策：是否重用现有专家、创建新专家或延迟决策。现有方法缺乏统计保证，无法确保决策的有效性和错误率控制。

Method: 将重用和创建新模型问题建模为条件（机制层面）差异的单侧序贯假设检验，中间设置无差异区间；延迟决策是两个betting e-process都未累积足够证据的状态。使用可预测判别器序列的可观测代理差异，通过重启的e-detector实现无窗口的最新性保证，在几何间隔的重启时间点上维护betting supermartingales银行（O(log t)内存复杂度）。

Result: 在合成多概念流、Electricity、Covertype以及具有重出现特性的INSECTS基准测试上，实例化的重启银行方法实现了零错误创建和切换后的零错误重用。在INSECTS-reoccurring数据集上达到0.675的准确率，匹配或超过已退役的窗口化启发式方法。

Conclusion: 成功将部署算法与保证算法合二为一，实现了零错误创建和零错误重用，同时保证了统计有效性和计算效率，为流数据专家模型管理提供了可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Streaming systems that maintain a pool of expert models must repeatedly decide whether to reuse an existing expert for arriving data, spawn a new one, or defer. We present a decision layer that makes all three outcomes statistically meaningful. Reuse and spawn are posed as one-sided sequential hypotheses on a conditional (mechanism-level) discrepancy, separated by an indifference zone; defer is exactly the state in which neither betting e-process has accumulated sufficient evidence. We prove finite-time anytime validity for the observable surrogate discrepancy of a predictable discriminator sequence, and an unconditional one-sided transfer to the population quantity in which each side's slack is the excess risk of a single discriminator; an empirically observed downward-bias regularity makes the spawn side exactly conservative. Recency without sacrificing the guarantee is obtained by a restarted e-detector: a bank of unwindowed betting supermartingales at geometrically spaced restart times (O(log t) memory), with the error budget spent over restart instances, which preserves lifetime anytime validity; spending over expert-creation order likewise controls multiplicity for unboundedly many experts. On synthetic multi-concept streams, Electricity, Covertype, and the recurrence-heavy INSECTS benchmark, the instance-accounted restarted bank achieves zero false spawns and zero false reuses after switches and matches or exceeds the retired windowed heuristic (INSECTS-reoccurring accuracy 0.675), making the deployed algorithm and the guaranteed algorithm one and the same.

</details>


### [123] [Reliable Neural Collapse Approximation for Open-World Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19890)
*Jia-Qi Lin,Yuangang Pan,Chang-Dong Wang,Haizhang Zhang,Ivor W. Tsang,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReNC的方法来解决开放世界测试时适应问题，通过利用神经崩溃作为结构先验来过滤分布外样本并优化原型，从而实现可靠的目标域适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的测试时适应方法在面对标签分布偏移（开放世界场景）时会失效。现有的方法无法有效区分和处理分布外样本，导致在目标域上的适应不可靠。

Method: 提出ReNC方法：1）利用神经崩溃作为结构先验，将预训练分类器权重作为源域原型；2）通过计算样本与原型的相似性来过滤OOD样本，实现可靠的模型更新；3）设计神经崩溃近似机制来优化原型，使其能逐渐适应目标域同时保持神经崩溃结构。

Result: 在多个开放世界基准数据集上的大量实验证明了ReNC方法的优越性。实证分析表明ReNC能更好地在目标域中保持神经崩溃相关特性，为解释可靠的OWTTA提供了有用证据。

Conclusion: ReNC方法通过神经崩溃先验成功解决了开放世界测试时适应的挑战，为模型设计提供了新的见解，并已开源代码便于后续研究。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods aim to bridge the domain gap between the source and target domains. However, traditional TTA methods become ineffective when the label distribution shift occurs, a challenge commonly referred to as an open-world scenario. In this paper, we introduce a new method named Reliable Neural Collapse approximation (ReNC) for Open-World Test-Time Adaptation (OWTTA). Specifically, we leverage neural collapse as a structural prior for reliable target-domain adaptation. Guided by this prior, we justify that the pre-trained classifier weights can serve as the prototypes of the source domain. By measuring the similarity between samples and prototypes, we filter out the Out-Of-Distribution~(OOD) samples for reliable updates. Furthermore, we propose a neural collapse approximation mechanism to refine these prototypes, ensuring they can gradually adapt to the target domain while maintaining the neural collapse structure. Extensive experiments on several open-world benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method. Our empirical analysis suggests that ReNC better preserves NC-related properties in the target domain, providing useful evidence for explaining reliable OWTTA and offering new insights for model design. Code is available at https://github.com/JiaqiLin-AI/ReNC.

</details>


### [124] [PETA:Parameter-Efficient Test-Time Adaptation for Virtual Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19906)
*Jia-Qi Lin,Yinghua Yao,Chang-Dong Wang,Yew-Soon Ong,Yuangang Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PETA框架，通过测试时自适应技术，仅更新约0.03%的模型参数（LayerNorm），即可将预训练的虚拟筛选模型定制化到特定蛋白口袋，在性能上超越预训练和全量微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于对比学习的虚拟筛选方法（如DrugCLIP）虽然加速了配体排序过程，但进一步提升性能通常需要重新训练整个模型，计算开销巨大，使得针对特定靶点的定制化效率低下。

Method: 将预训练模型的靶点定制化建模为测试时自适应问题。针对目标口袋，通过分子扩散和化学有效性过滤构建口袋特异性的负样本，并利用嵌入空间混合技术将负样本向参考配体靠近以增加排序难度；采用排序目标函数重点抑制可能污染顶级结果的高分无效候选物。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，该方法仅需更新约0.03%的模型参数（LayerNorm参数），即可超越预训练模型和全量重新训练基线模型的性能。

Conclusion: PETA框架证明了测试时自适应是解决虚拟筛选模型靶点定制化的有效途径，实现了参数高效、性能优越且计算成本低廉的轻量级自适应方案。

Abstract: Accurately ranking active ligands for a target protein pocket from massive chemical libraries remains a central challenge in virtual screening. DrugCLIP and its recent extensions substantially accelerate this process by encoding protein pockets and molecules into a shared embedding space. Despite this progress, further performance improvements typically require retraining the entire model, incurring substantial computational overhead and making target-specific customization inefficient. In this work, we formulate the specialization of pretrained virtual screening models to individual pockets as a test-time adaptation problem and propose PETA, a parameter-efficient framework that directly adapts pretrained model at test time. Given a target pocket, PETA constructs pocket-specific negatives through molecular diffusion and chemical validity filtering, and further moves them toward the reference ligand retrieved from structural databases via embedding-space mixup to create more challenging ranking tasks. A ranking objective then places greater emphasis on suppressing high-scoring invalid candidates that could contaminate the top-ranked screening results, providing structured supervision for lightweight adaptation. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that this lightweight, pocket-specific adaptation outperforms both pretrained and fully retrained baselines while updating only the LayerNorm parameters, which account for approximately $0.03\%$ of the full model.

</details>


### [125] [Multi-Source Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19914)
*Chuansen Peng,Yifan Xia,Jinshan Zhong,Xiaojing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MS-WDRO多源Wasserstein分布鲁棒图学习框架，通过加权Wasserstein重心融合异构源域数据，解决了样本稀缺场景下的网络拓扑推断问题，在神经影像等任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络拓扑推断中目标域样本稀缺而源域数据丰富，但现有欧氏平均方法在源域异构性增大时性能急剧下降，会将不同几何结构坍缩为有偏差的共识，亟需能处理异构性的融合方法。

Method: 提出MS-WDRO框架：(1)利用Wasserstein度量的分布保持特性，通过加权Wasserstein重心融合异构源域；(2)构建模糊球应对残余不确定性；(3)最小化最坏情况风险得到正则化拉普拉斯估计器，通过收敛的ADMM算法求解；(4)将求解器展开为可微架构实现端到端训练，实现超参数数据自适应校准。

Result: 建立了非渐近理论保证：经验重心的有限样本浓度界、证明朴素聚合次优性的池化偏误下界、以参数速率衰减且仅对源数量有对数依赖的样本外超额风险界。在合成基准和多站点ABIDE I神经影像数据集上，MS-WDRO在图恢复、样本效率和下游诊断效用方面持续优于七个基线，在样本稀缺场景下优势最显著。

Conclusion: MS-WDRO通过几何原则性的Wasserstein重心融合成功处理了源域异构性，提供了理论保证和实际性能提升，为样本稀缺场景下的多源图学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Network topology inference from graph signals is central to graph signal processing with applications in neuroscience, sensor, and social networks. In practice, target-domain samples are scarce while heterogeneous source-domain data are abundant. Fusing these sources is challenging: Euclidean averaging works for homogeneous sources but degrades sharply as inter-source divergence grows, collapsing distinct geometries into an inflated, biased consensus. We exploit the Wasserstein metric's distribution-preserving properties to counter heterogeneity while preserving each source's intrinsic geometry. We propose MS-WDRO, a multi-source Wasserstein distributionally robust graph learning framework that fuses heterogeneous sources via their weighted Wasserstein barycenter, a geometrically principled nominal distribution, then builds an ambiguity ball around it to hedge residual uncertainty. Minimizing worst-case risk yields a tractable regularized Laplacian estimator solved efficiently via a provably convergent ADMM scheme. We establish non-asymptotic guarantees: a finite-sample concentration bound for the empirical barycenter, a pooling bias lower bound proving naive aggregation is suboptimal, and an out-of-sample excess risk bound decaying at a parametric rate with only logarithmic dependence on source count. To calibrate hyperparameters governing robustness, sparsity, and source fusion, we unroll the solver into a differentiable architecture trained end-to-end, achieving data-adaptive calibration beyond cross-validation while retaining interpretability. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and the multi-site ABIDE~I neuroimaging dataset show MS-WDRO consistently outperforms seven baselines in graph recovery, sample efficiency, and downstream diagnostic utility, with the largest gains in the sample-scarce regime.

</details>


### [126] [Auditing Recorded Predictive Lead Service-Line Classifications Against Physical Verification: A Statewide Study of New York](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19922)
*Muhammad Sarmad Sohail*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究审查了纽约州153个地方政府使用预测模型确定供水管线材质的情况，发现49%的地方（覆盖57%的筛选地址）仅记录单一值，其中7个地方的数据存在矛盾。纽约市作为最大案例，模型分类的43,215个地址全部标记为"已知其他"，铅管率为零，而模型预测的群体建筑年代更新，经年代调整后仍发现潜在铅管约1,150-1,450条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国铅铜法规修订允许供水企业使用预测模型确定供水管线材质而非实地检测，但模型准确性缺乏验证。纽约州发布了各地址的分类方法数据，为评估模型分类的可靠性提供了机会，特别是针对铅管这一公共健康关键问题。

Method: 研究筛查了纽约州153个使用模型分类至少100个地址的地方政府数据。通过比较同一供水系统内采用不同方法（模型分类与物理验证）的人群，识别数据异常。运用年代感知估计方法，控制建筑年代变量后评估模型预测群体中的预期铅管数量。

Result: 75个地方政府（49%）仅记录单一值，其中7个与自身验证数据矛盾。纽约市模型分类的43,215个地址全部标记为"已知其他"，铅管数和未知数均为零，95%上限为0.0085%。模型预测群体建筑年代中位数为1984年（对比1930年）。固定年代后，基于记录分类发现铅管率4.3-31.9%，物理验证1.5-14.5%，模型分类为0。六个年代感知估计显示预期铅管1,150-1,450条。7,782个地址位于1940年前建筑，且发现公共侧判定直接复制了客户侧模型输出。

Conclusion: 预测模型分类结果存在系统性异常，部分地方政府的数据与自身验证矛盾。纽约市案例显示模型分类可能产生不合常理的零铅管率，考虑到建筑年代因素和物理验证对比结果，模型可能低估了铅管数量。研究揭示了模型分类方法的潜在缺陷，建议加强监管和验证机制。

Abstract: Under the US Lead and Copper Rule Revisions, a utility may determine a service line's material with a predictive model instead of inspecting it. New York State publishes, per address, which method was used. Almost no address carries both a model classification and a physical verification, so the check is between populations within a utility rather than paired addresses. We screen all 153 New York localities that classified at least 100 addresses this way. Seventy-five (49%), covering 125,990 addresses or 57% of those screened, record one value. Zero variance alone is not misconduct: 68 of the 75 match their own verification or have too little to test. Seven are contradicted by their own crews, six beyond any sampling explanation. Five are boroughs of New York City, which file as one system; one is East Rochester, 550 km away. New York City is the largest case: a predictive model is the recorded basis for 43,215 addresses, and on all of them the recorded material is "Known Other". The city records "Unknown" on 121,779 addresses, 1,880 already excavated, and lead on 120,692. In the model bucket both counts are zero, and the 95% upper bound on the rate is 0.0085%. Across the rest of New York the same method records lead or the hedge "Unknown but could be lead" on 12.21% of 176,888 addresses, a comparison whose weaknesses we report. The model-cleared population is newer, median year built 1984 against 1930, and construction era accounts for about a third of the gap and not the rest: holding era fixed, records-based classification finds lead at 4.3-31.9%, physical verification at 1.5-14.5%, the model in no era. Six era-aware estimators place the expected lead lines among them at 1,150-1,450. Two findings need no comparison: 7,782 of these addresses are in pre-1940 buildings, and the archived 2025 snapshot shows the public-side determination was copied from a customer-side model output.

</details>


### [127] [G-MARK: Grounded Multi-Agent Reasoning for Cooperative Driving via Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19964)
*Bhavya Gupta,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出G-MARK框架，通过构建溯源感知知识图谱显式表示多车协作中的观测来源和冲突，显著提升自动驾驶在遮挡场景下的推理与决策性能，通信开销大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统在部分可观测环境下运行，安全关键物体可能被遮挡或仅对邻近联网车辆可见。车车协作可降低不确定性，但现有方法将多智能体证据压缩为潜在特征或隐式多模态状态，导致观测来源、自车可见性、冲突证据对决策的影响被模糊化。

Method: 提出G-MARK，一种基于显式溯源感知知识图谱（KGs）的多智能体推理框架。该框架将协作式以对象为中心的观测转换为知识图谱，保留对象假设及其来源归因、自车与伙伴车辆的可见性、不确定性、冲突、空间关系及规划相关上下文，并从KGs中提取共享特征表示，支持轻量化任务头进行对象推理、运动预测、控制选择和轨迹预测。

Result: 在遮挡推理准确率上比最先进基线提升42.2%，控制选择误差降低13.1%，轨迹规划准确率相当，结构化通信负载降低25.6倍。

Conclusion: G-MARK通过显式、可溯源的知识图谱表示，有效解决了多车协作中的信息模糊化问题，在显著提升关键性能指标的同时大幅降低了通信开销，为自动驾驶安全决策提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems must operate under partial observability, where safety-critical objects may be occluded or visible only to neighboring connected vehicles. Vehicle-to-vehicle cooperation can reduce this uncertainty, but existing cooperative driving methods often compress multi-agent evidence into latent features or hidden multimodal states. As a result, they obscure which agent observed each object, whether the object is visible to the ego vehicle, and how conflicting evidence affects downstream decisions. We propose G-MARK, a grounded multi-agent reasoning framework that converts cooperative object-centric observations into explicit provenance-aware knowledge graphs (KGs). The resulting KGs preserve object hypotheses together with their source attribution, ego-versus-partner visibility, uncertainty, conflicts, spatial relations, and planning-relevant context. G-MARK then derives a shared feature representation from these KGs, enabling lightweight task heads to support object reasoning, motion prediction, control selection, and trajectory forecasting. Compared with the state-of-the-art baseline, GMARK improves occlusion reasoning accuracy by 42.2%, reduces control-selection error by 13.1%, and achieves comparable trajectory-planning accuracy with a 25.6x smaller structured communication payload. Our code is available at https://github.com/bhavyagupta98/g-mark.

</details>


### [128] [Green BOA: Determining the environmental break-even point for ML-based data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19994)
*Caterina Doglioni,Akshat Gupta,Thomas Elliott,Hanzila Hussain,Sanjiban Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了基于机器学习的数据压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的盈亏平衡点，发现需要权衡ML训练推理的碳排放成本与减少存储带来的碳节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习应用的普及，其在环境保护方面的影响日益受到关注。本研究旨在探索ML技术在数据压缩领域的环境可持续性，特别是ML训练和推理产生的碳排放与减少存储需求带来的碳排放节省之间的权衡关系。

Method: 以基于机器学习的无损压缩算法为例，估算ML训练和推理所需基础设施的碳排放当量，同时计算因减少磁盘存储需求而节省的碳排放当量，通过对比两者确定盈亏平衡点。

Result: 研究提供了ML数据压缩算法环境成本的量化分析框架，确定了碳排放投入与节省的平衡条件，为评估ML技术在绿色计算中的应用价值提供了依据。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的数据压缩技术需要达到特定的使用规模和时间才能实现环境效益的正向回报，这为ML技术在可持续发展中的应用决策提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We summarise the outcome of two summer internship projects based at the University of Manchester, focused on the break-even point in terms of environmental sustainability for ML-based data compression algorithms. Using the example of a ML-based lossless compression algorithm, we compare estimates for the carbon-equivalent of the infrastructure needed for ML training and inference with the carbon-equivalent savings from reduced disk storage requirements, and discuss their break-even point.

</details>


### [129] [Scale-Aware Pretraining of Time Series Foundation Models via Multi-Patch Token Alignment and Hybrid Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20005)
*Taihua Chen,Xiang Ma,Yixin Zhang,Tailin Zhan,Manyu Sun,Lizhen Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SATS方法，通过尺度感知令牌对齐机制和混合掩码策略解决时间序列基础模型跨异构数据集预训练中的频率差异问题，在LSTF基准上实现MSE提升9.2%，模型效率提高65.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨异构数据集预训练时间序列基础模型的方法在处理不同采样频率时存在两难：要么使用数据集特定的patch大小和独立FFN导致表示碎片化，要么强制固定patch大小而忽略内在时间变化。这促使作者设计一种能够统一处理不同频率数据的方法。

Method: 提出SATS方法，核心包含两个创新：(1)尺度感知令牌对齐机制，将patch大小显式视为尺度概念，通过对比学习启发的对齐正则化项在保持不同建模能力的同时跨尺度对齐表示空间；(2)混合掩码策略，结合随机掩码和连续掩码以捕获多尺度时间结构。

Result: 在LSTF基准测试中，MSE指标相对竞争基线提升9.2%，GIFT-Eval MASE指标提升8.3%；模型效率相对先进基线提高65.6%，同时持续达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SATS成功解决了时间序列预训练中频率异构性挑战，证明了尺度感知对齐和混合掩码策略的有效性，为时间序列基础模型的扩展性和效率提升提供了新范式。

Abstract: Pretraining time series foundation models across heterogeneous datasets necessitates effective handling of varying sampling frequencies. Current methods either employ dataset-specific patch sizes and separate FFNs, leading to fragmented representations, or enforce a fixed patch size that neglects inherent temporal variations. To address this, we propose SATS, featuring a scale-aware token alignment mechanism that treats patch size as an explicit notion of scale. By incorporating a contrastive-inspired alignment regularizer, SATS aligns representation spaces across scales while preserving distinct modeling capacities. Furthermore, a hybrid masking strategy combining random and contiguous masking is introduced to capture multi-scale temporal structures. Experimental results on LSTF benchmarks demonstrate that SATS achieves a 9.2% improvement in MSE and an 8.3% gain in GIFT-Eval MASE compared to competitive baselines. Notably, SATS consistently delivers SOTA performance while achieving a 65.6% increase in model efficiency over advanced baselines, highlighting its effectiveness and scalability in time series pretraining.

</details>


### [130] [Systematic Evaluation of TabPFN-TS for Zero-Shot Probabilistic Heat Load Forecasting in District Heating Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20024)
*Ben Spoek,Karim K. Ben Hicham,Kai Derzsi,Philipp Althaus,Alexander Mitsos,Dirk Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了TabPFN-TS在区域供热网络热负荷预测中的表现，发现其基于合成数据的预训练方法无需真实数据即可达到与Chronos-2相当的预测精度，且具有更好的校准性能，同时提出了多分辨率残差校正预测器以改善长期预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域供热能源枢纽需要可靠的热负荷预测来实现高效运行调度。传统工作流程在系统特定模型上训练历史数据，当网络通过新消费者、改造或改变运行机制而发生变化时，训练负担会变得沉重。零样本时间序列基础模型和上下文预测提供了一个有前景的替代方案：它们可以在推理时从最近的观测中进行适应，而不是通过重复训练。

Method: 系统评估TabPFN-TS与时间序列基础模型和训练的机器学习基线在区域供热网络概率热负荷预测中的表现。分析协变量选择、上下文长度、时间分辨率和预测范围对代表性运行周的影响，在完整年份上验证选定的配置，并在第二个网络上测试可迁移性。最终提出多分辨率残差校正预测器，结合低频基础预测器和短视残差预测器。

Result: 确定了每小时24小时预测配合12周滚动上下文和环境温度作为简约高性能配置；更长的上下文窗口不能提高准确性。TabPFN-TS在确定性精度上接近Chronos-2，在主数据集上达到CVRMSE值13.06%对12.48%，在日排名比较中处于临界差异阈值内。虽然Chronos-2实现了最低的全年总误差，但TabPFN-TS显示出更好的经验校准。

Conclusion: TabPFN-TS证明了基于合成数据预训练的可行性，避免了直接预训练-测试重叠问题，同时保持了与在真实时序数据上预训练的基础模型相当的性能。多分辨率残差校正预测器为改善长期规划精度提供了有效方法。这为零样本预测在动态变化的区域供热网络中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: District heating energy hubs require reliable heat load forecasts for efficient operational scheduling. Conventional forecasting workflows train system-specific models on historical data, which can become burdensome when networks change through new consumers, retrofits, or changing operating regimes. Zero-shot time-series foundation models and in-context forecasting offer a promising alternative: they can adapt at inference time from recent observations rather than by repeated retraining. This study systematically evaluates TabPFN-TS against time-series foundation models and trained machine-learning baselines for probabilistic heat load forecasting in district heating networks. Unlike foundation models pretrained on large collections of real time series, TabPFN-TS relies on synthetic pretraining data, which avoids direct pretraining-test overlap but raises the question of whether the learned prior captures district heating dynamics. We analyze covariate choice, context length, temporal resolution, and prediction horizon on representative operating weeks, validate the selected configuration over a full year, and test transferability on a second network. The results identify hourly 24-hour forecasting with a 12-week rolling context and ambient temperature as a parsimonious high-performing configuration; longer context windows do not improve accuracy. TabPFN-TS remains close to Chronos-2 in deterministic accuracy, reaching CVRMSE values of 13.06% versus 12.48% on the main dataset, and lies within the critical-difference threshold in the daily-rank comparison. Although Chronos-2 achieves the lowest aggregate full-year error, TabPFN-TS shows better empirical calibration. Finally, the diagnostic findings motivate a Multi-Resolution Residual-Correction Forecaster that combines a low-frequency Base Forecaster with a short-horizon Residual Forecaster to improve longer-horizon planning accuracy.

</details>


### [131] [CLaST: Context-aware Contrastive VAE for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20025)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Petr Sokerin,Vitaliy Pozdnyakov,Ilya Kuleshov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLaST是一种基于VAE的概率多变量时间序列预测框架，通过对比损失函数学习保持观测间上下文相似性的嵌入表示，在短期和长期预测任务中均显著超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成概率预测模型难以捕捉内部时间依赖关系，导致潜在表示的表达能力受限，影响预测性能。

Method: 提出CLaST框架，这是一种VAE架构，核心创新在于设计了对比损失函数，使模型学习能够保持观测间上下文相似性的嵌入表示，从而更好地捕捉时间序列的内在结构。

Result: 在9个广泛采用的基准数据集上的实验表明，CLaST持续超越强基线方法：短期预测中CRPS提升最高16.4%，NMAE提升最高14.4%；长期预测中CRPS提升最高48.6%，NMAE提升最高25.1%。

Conclusion: CLaST通过对比学习有效增强了潜在表示的表达能力，在概率时间序列预测任务中取得了显著的性能提升，验证了保持上下文相似性对捕捉时间依赖关系的重要性。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are widely used for time series forecasting in domains such as energy systems, finance, medicine, and transportation. In recent years, deep generative models have shown strong results on probabilistic forecasting, yet many conventional approaches struggle to capture internal temporal dependencies, leading to latent representations with limited expressive power. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{CLaST}, a VAE framework for probabilistic multivariate time series forecasting. Unlike existing generative models, CLaST learns embeddings that preserve contextual similarity between observations through our contrastive loss function. Experiments across nine widely adopted benchmarks demonstrate that CLaST consistently surpasses strong baseline methods. In short-term forecasting tasks, our approach achieves improvements of up to $16.4\%$ in CRPS and $14.4\%$ in NMAE over the second-best method. Furthermore, in long-term prediction CLaST attains superior overall performance, exceeding the second-best method by up to $48.6\%$ and $25.1\%$ in CRPS and NMAE, respectively.

</details>


### [132] [An Inclusive and Lightweight Approach to Federated Continual Learning for Cultural Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20038)
*Ioannis Theologitis,Debin Meng,Stylianos Eleftheriadis,Vasileios Lolis,Konstantinos Votis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FedCurv-DR，一种轻量级的联邦持续学习策略，专门用于文化遗产和数字人文领域的分布式、持续演化的数据场景，在减少遗忘的同时平衡了性能、公平性和能源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能可以支持文化遗产和数字人文领域的大规模检索和分析，但文化遗产数据通常分布在不同机构，受所有权和访问限制约束，并且随时间持续演化。这种分布式、顺序性的数据特点需要一种能够在不共享原始数据的情况下进行学习的方案。联邦持续学习(FCL)非常适合这种场景，但需要解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题以及联邦学习中的通信和计算开销问题。

Method: 提出了FedCurv-DR，一种基于正则化的轻量级联邦持续学习策略。该方法在客户端和经验之间累积参数重要性估计，以保护已学习的知识，同时仅在固定间隔更新这些估计，以最小化通信和计算开销。通过正则化机制来防止灾难性遗忘，并通过周期性更新策略来降低系统开销。

Result: 在WikiArt图像数据集上进行了实验评估，采用流派分类任务并模拟风格演化的持续学习场景。评估指标包括性能、能源和公平性指标。结果表明FedCurv-DR能够减少遗忘，并在性能、公平性和能源效率之间取得良好的平衡。

Conclusion: FedCurv-DR为文化遗产领域的可持续AI提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够在保护数据隐私的前提下，有效地处理分布式和持续演化的文化遗产数据，同时平衡多个重要目标：减少灾难性遗忘、提高公平性、降低能源消耗，这对于文化遗产和数字人文领域的AI应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence can support cultural heritage and digital humanities through large-scale retrieval and analysis of digitized collections. However, cultural heritage data are often distributed across institutions, constrained by ownership and access restrictions, and continuously evolving over time. Federated Continual Learning (FCL) is well suited to this setting, as it enables models to learn from distributed and sequential data without sharing raw collections. In this paper, we propose FedCurv-DR, a lightweight, regularisation-based FCL strategy. The method accumulates parameter-importance estimates across clients and experiences to protect learned knowledge, while updating them only at fixed intervals to minimize communication and computation overhead. We evaluate FedCurv-DR in a continual learning scenario using the WikiArt image dataset for genre classification with evolving styles, reporting performance, energy, and fairness metrics. Our results show that FedCurv- DR reduces forgetting and balances performance, fairness, and energy efficiency for sustainable AI in cultural heritage.

</details>


### [133] [End-to-end Early Classification of Time Series in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20044)
*Aurélien Renault,Alexis Bondu,Antoine Cornuéjols,Vincent Lemaire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DQeND方法，首次系统比较了可分离设计与端到端方法在非平稳环境下时间序列早期分类的性能，证明端到端联合学习更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序早期分类(ECTS)方法假设环境平稳，且采用分类与触发决策独立优化的可分离设计，这种假设在概念漂移场景下限制了模型的适应能力。本文挑战这一范式，研究非平稳条件下的ECTS问题。

Method: 基于强化学习提出DQeND统一架构，联合学习表示、分类和触发决策三个模块，首次在受控漂移场景下系统比较可分离与端到端方法，并与最先进可分离基线保持直接可比性。

Result: 在多种漂移场景下，DQeND表现出强鲁棒性，持续优于可分离基线；消融实验表明联合更新表示模块和决策模块对性能提升至关重要。

Conclusion: 端到端学习能够为动态环境下的时序早期分类提供更好的自适应能力，激励进一步研究可分离设计的替代方案。

Abstract: Early Classification of Time Series (ECTS) requires making accurate decisions as early as possible in inherently online and evolving environments. Yet, most existing methods assume stationarity and rely on separable designs, where classification and triggering are optimized independently, an assumption that fundamentally limits their adaptability under drift. In this work, we challenge this paradigm and study ECTS under non-stationary conditions. We provide the first systematic comparison between separable and end-to-end approaches across controlled drifting scenarios. Building on Reinforcement Learning, we introduce DQeND, a unified architecture that jointly learns representation, classification, and triggering decisions, while remaining directly comparable to state-of-the-art separable baselines. Across a wide range of drifts, DQeND demonstrates strong robustness across various non-stationary scenarios, consistently outperforming separable baselines. An ablation study further highlights that jointly updating representation and decision modules is critical to these gains. Overall, our results indicate that end-to-end learning can offer improved adaptation capabilities for ECTS in dynamic environments, and motivate further investigation of alternatives to separable designs.

</details>


### [134] [DecoVAE: a Lightweight Interpretable Trend-Seasonal VAE Framework for Efficient Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20052)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DecoVAE，一种轻量级可解释的趋势-季节性变分自编码器框架，通过在时域和频域分别建模趋势和季节分量，在显著降低模型复杂度的同时实现了更优的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率时间序列预测面临挑战，主要因为趋势和季节性动态具有不同特性需要专门方法建模。现有方法往往无法捕捉这些分量的独特内在属性，缺乏可解释性，或存在严重的内存和运行时开销问题。

Method: DecoVAE框架通过应用领域特定归纳偏置将时间序列显式分解为趋势和季节分量。趋势流通过对潜在轨迹施加微分正则化器来强制结构平滑性（类似于Hodrick-Prescott滤波器）；季节流通过复数高斯VAE在频域中操作，原生捕捉周期模式的幅度和相位。

Result: 在七个真实世界基准数据集上的广泛评估显示，DecoVAE持续优于强基线方法。短期预测中CRPS降低达14.96%，NMAE降低达23.30%；长期预测中分别降低达52.68%和26.51%。同时，模型权重减少达93%，速度提升达74%（相比次优方法）。

Conclusion: DecoVAE成功实现了准确性提升与计算效率的平衡，证明了通过显式分解和领域特定归纳偏置可以有效解决时间序列预测中趋势-季节性建模的挑战，为实际应用提供了高效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting remains challenging, largely because modeling distinct trend and seasonal dynamics requires specialized approaches. Existing methods often fail to capture the unique inner properties of these components, lack interpretability, or suffer from heavy memory and runtime overhead. To address these limitations, we propose DecoVAE, a lightweight interpretable trend-seasonal VAE framework that explicitly decomposes time series into trend and seasonal components by applying domain-specific inductive biases. The trend stream enforces structural smoothness using a differential regularizer on the latent trajectory, analogous to the Hodrick-Prescott filter. Concurrently, the seasonal stream operates in the frequency domain via a complex Gaussian VAE, natively capturing the amplitude and phase of periodic patterns. Extensive evaluations across seven real-world benchmarks show that DecoVAE consistently outperforms strong baselines. It achieves reductions of up to 14.96\% in CRPS and 23.30\% in NMAE for short-term forecasting, and up to 52.68\% and 26.51\% for long-term horizons. Crucially, DecoVAE yields these accuracy gains while remaining highly efficient, reducing model weight by up to 93\% and accelerating speed by up to 74\% compared to the second-best method.

</details>


### [135] [Let's Scale Step by Step: Compute-Efficient Hyperparameter Transfer for Large-Scale Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20061)
*Nayeon Kim,Hojin Lee,Yunju Bak,Jaesun Park,Boseop Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的超参数迁移框架，通过在小规模代理模型上确定最佳学习率，成功预测并验证了155B参数大规模MoE模型在万亿token训练规模下的最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合专家模型（MoE）在扩展模型容量时具有计算效率优势，但在极端规模下（大模型尺寸和海量token预算），通过传统扫参方法优化学习率等超参数在计算上不可行，亟需一种计算高效的超参数优化方法。

Method: 提出两步超参数迁移框架：1) 针对采用多头潜在注意力（MLA）和Muon优化器的MoE架构，制定了最大更新参数化（μP）适配方案，实现最优学习率在宽度缩放模型间的迁移；2) 建立预测性缩放定律，通过对小代理模型在有限预算下的最优值进行线性回归，将学习率外推至大规模训练范围。

Result: 在小模型上得到的最优值能以高保真度（R²=0.95）外推至万亿token训练规模；应用该方法从头预训练155B总参数、17B激活参数的基础模型，实现了稳定训练，验证了用最小消融成本准确预测全规模目标模型最优配置的可行性。

Conclusion: 证明了在小规模代理模型上的训练足以确定大规模MoE模型长时间训练的最优学习率，显著降低了超参数优化的计算成本，为大规模MoE模型的高效训练提供了有效方法论。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures significantly expand model capacity without a proportional increase in computational cost. However, optimizing their hyperparameters---particularly the learning rate---at extreme scales of both model size and token budget via sweeping remains computationally prohibitive. In this paper, we propose a compute-efficient, two-step hyperparameter transfer framework that estimates optimal learning rates for training large MoE models by transferring them across scaling model widths, and subsequently extrapolating to trillion-token horizons. First, we formulate a Maximal Update Parameterization ($μ$P) adaptation for MoE architectures utilizing Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) and the Muon optimizer, demonstrating that optimal learning rates transfer consistently across width-scaled models. Second, we extend this transferability along the token dimension by establishing a predictive scaling law. By applying linear regression to the optimal values derived from small proxy models on limited budgets, we successfully extrapolate the ideal learning rate to massive training horizons (e.g., 10 trillion tokens) with high fidelity ($R^2=0.95$). Consequently, this indicates that proxy training on small models is sufficient to determine the optimal learning rate for the extensive training of large-scale MoEs. We apply the proposed methodology to pretrain our foundation model (155B total, 17B active parameters) from scratch, and the stable training and evaluation results validate that optimal configurations for full-scale target models can be accurately predicted with minimal ablation costs.

</details>


### [136] [Orthogonal JEPA: Factorized Predictive States for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20065)
*Taoyong Cui,Pheng Ann Heng,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了\method框架，通过正交预测分解将复杂系统状态分解为多个组件，解决了标准JEPAs在处理复杂系统时对主导信号分配冗余容量而忽略次要信号的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）通过单一目标嵌入和预测路径组织所有可预测内容，在复杂系统中这种单一状态会将冗余容量分配给主导信号，而对次要预测结构提供弱或冲突的梯度，导致表示能力受限。

Method: 提出基于正交预测分解的潜在世界建模框架：学习基矩阵将每个目标状态分解为多个组件，使用专用预测分支从共享上下文表示估计每个组件。引入预测回归保持状态合成所需的因子幅度、正交性目标阻止重复方向、因子活动正则化维持投影目标的变异、在线方差正则化防止坐标级编码器崩塌。预测组件合成完整潜在状态，可应用于读出、解码、规划或自回归展开。

Result: 在控制视觉、单细胞转录组学、纵向健康记录、连续控制和分子动力学等多样化任务上的实验表明，该方法在表示质量、预测、规划和长时程稳定性方面表现良好。

Conclusion: 正交预测分解框架成功克服了标准JEPAs的局限性，为复杂系统建模提供了更有效的潜在状态学习机制，且该方法在时间未来、空间隐藏或同一系统的部分观察等不同场景下均适用。

Abstract: World models construct latent states that support prediction, planning, and reasoning about an underlying system. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) offer a direct way to learn such states by predicting targets in representation space instead of reconstructing every detail of the observation. Standard JEPAs, however, organize all predictable content through one target embedding and one prediction pathway. In complex systems, this monolithic state can allocate redundant capacity to dominant signals while providing weak or conflicting gradients to less dominant predictive structure. We introduce \method, a latent world-modeling framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization. Learned basis matrices analyze each target state into multiple components, and a dedicated prediction branch estimates each component from a shared context representation. Predictive regression preserves the factor magnitudes required for state synthesis, an orthogonality objective discourages repeated directions, factor-activity regularization maintains variation in projected targets, and online variance regularization discourages coordinate-wise encoder collapse. Predicted components are synthesized into a complete latent state that can be used by a readout, decoder, planner, or autoregressive rollout. The same predictive-state mechanism applies when the target is temporally future, spatially hidden, or another partial observation of the same system. Experiments on controlled vision, single-cell transcriptomics, longitudinal health records, continuous control, and molecular dynamics evaluate representation quality, forecasting, planning, and long-horizon stability.

</details>


### [137] [SAE-Xplainers: Rule-Based Feature Interpretation for Extreme Earth Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20117)
*Hugo Porta,Emanuele Dalsasso,Chang Xu,Theo Gnassounou,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于地理位置调制的稀疏自编码器(SAE)和SAE-Xplainer解释器，用于提升极端地球事件预测模型的可解释性，在火灾预测、热带气旋和大气河流检测任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天气气候数据集为极端地球事件建模提供了新机遇，但深度学习模型在业务场景中的应用受限，主要原因是缺乏模型可解释性。虽然稀疏自编码器(SAE)在文本和图像模态中能有效提取人类可理解的概念，但由于天气气候数据的特殊性，将其应用于极端地球事件分析仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出两个核心创新：(i)基于地理位置的SAE输入调制机制，用于捕获环境模式的局部语义含义；(ii)SAE-Xplainer集成规则解释器，用于解释从复杂多模态环境预测因子中提取的高维特征。该方法在三种极端地球事件上进行评估：火灾预测、热带气旋检测和大气河流检测。

Result: 实验表明：SAE输入调制改善了重建性能和特征利用率；SAE-Xplainer能够将复杂气候模式展开为与科学文献一致的人类可理解规则，实现忠实解释，同时支持特征吸收的识别。

Conclusion: 本研究成功解决了稀疏自编码器在天气气候数据中的应用挑战，为极端地球事件预测提供了可解释的深度学习解决方案，有助于推动AI技术在气象气候业务中的实际部署。

Abstract: The emergence of large-scale Weather and Climate (W&C) datasets offers new opportunities for modeling extreme Earth events (ExEE) and their impacts using deep learning. However, their adoption in operational settings remains limited by the lack of models' interpretability. While for conventional text and image modalities, tools such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have proven effective for extracting human-understandable concepts, their use for the analysis of ExEE remains challenging due to the nature of W&C data. To address this, we introduce (i) a geographic location-based modulation of the inputs of SAE to capture the local semantic meaning of environmental patterns, and (ii) an ensemble of rule-based SAE-Xplainers to interpret the resulting high-dimensional features derived from complex, multi-modal environmental predictors. We evaluate our method on three ExEE types: the prediction of fires, and the detection of tropical cyclones and atmospheric rivers. We show that SAE input modulation improves both reconstruction performance and feature utilization, and that our SAE-Xplainers enable faithful interpretation of complex climatic patterns by unfolding them into human-understandable rules that are consistent with the scientific literature, while also supporting the identification of feature absorption.

</details>


### [138] [Evaluating Neural Cartographic Relief Shading for Urban Environments: A Downtown Calgary Study Using High-Resolution DEM and DSM Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20149)
*Emmanuel Stefanakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于神经网络的山体阴影方法（Eduard系统）在密集城市环境中的表现，与传统分析方法进行比较，发现虽然该系统主要针对山地地形训练，但通过参数调优仍可在城市环境中产生有价值的制图效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验原本为模拟瑞士风格山地地形阴影而开发的神经网络系统Eduard是否能够适应高度建筑化的城市环境。由于该系统并非为建筑物、桥梁、街道、树木等城市基础设施设计，核心问题在于通过参数调整是否能够产生视觉上强有力、制图上有用甚至优于传统分析方法的结果。

Method: 采用卡尔加里市中心的高分辨率数字高程模型(DEM)和数字表面模型(DSM)数据，对比三种山体阴影方法：单方向分析山体阴影、多方向分析山体阴影，以及Eduard神经网络系统生成的山体阴影。实验重点调整地形类型、微观和宏观综合、以及平坦区域细节参数，同时保持大尺度阴影风格不变。

Result: 研究发现：在部分情况下神经网络方法能够产生优于传统方法的制图效果；分析型山体阴影在某些方面仍然更可靠；神经网络方法因其对高山地形的训练偏向性而在某些城市特征表现上失败。研究识别了三种方法各自的优势和局限性领域。

Conclusion: 结论表明，针对自然地貌设计的神经方法可以被适配到高度建筑化的城市环境中，但效果有限。研究呼吁未来应针对城市地貌阴影进行专门的模型训练和评估，而非简单地借用山地地形训练的模型。

Abstract: This article explores the performance of analytical and neural-based hillshading methods in a dense urban environment using high-resolution digital elevation model (DEM) and digital surface model (DSM) data for downtown Calgary. The study compares single-direction and multi-direction analytical hillshading with relief shading generated in Eduard, a machine-learning system originally developed to emulate Swiss-style shaded relief trained primarily on mountainous landscapes. Because Eduard was not designed for buildings, bridges, streets, trees, and other urban infrastructures, the central question is not whether it perfectly reproduces urban morphology, but whether parameter tuning can nevertheless produce visually strong, cartographically useful, and in some cases superior results when compared with conventional analytical methods. The analysis focuses especially on terrain type, micro and macro generalization, and flat-area detail parameters, while keeping the large-scale shading style constant throughout the neural experiments. The article is structured as an exploratory comparison rather than a benchmark of universal best practice. It aims to identify where analytical hillshading remains more reliable, where Eduard offers unexpected strengths, and where neural shading fails because of its training bias toward alpine terrain. The study contributes to current work on terrain representation by testing whether a neural approach designed for natural landforms can be adapted to a highly built urban setting, and it concludes by arguing for future model training and evaluation specifically targeted at urban relief shading.

</details>


### [139] [Ask Self, Ask Others: Relation Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20172)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Relation，一种替代注意力机制的token混合原语，通过显式组织Self和Exchange关系来推导信息流，在多个规模上优于多头注意力，且FlashRelation实现了3.6-4.4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统注意力机制直接从成对分数推导归一化的信息流，缺乏显式的关係组织。作者希望引入一种关系优先的视角，先组织成对的证据为显式的Self和Exchange关系，然后再推导信息流。

Method: 提出Relation token混合原语，首先将成对证据组织为显式的Self和Exchange关系，然后推导信息流。基于这种关系组织，设计了Full Relation、FlashRelation、Linear Relation、Hybrid Relation和KV风格的Relation Cache等多种变体。

Result: 在约10M、30M、100M参数的解码器模型上，Full Relation在所有三个规模上都实现了比MHA更低的最终验证NLL。FlashRelation比实体化的Full Relation实现快3.60-4.41倍，在生产工作负载中达到PyTorch FlashAttention吞吐量的76.4-84.9%。使用75% Linear Relation层的Hybrid Relation实现了强语言建模质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持关系优先的token混合视角：先询问Self，再询问Others，然后让Flow跟随Relation。这表明显式的关係组织可以有效替代传统的注意力机制。

Abstract: Attention directly derives normalized information flow from pairwise scores. We introduce Relation, an alternative token-mixing primitive that first organizes pairwise evidence into explicit Self and Exchange relations and derives information flow afterward. This relational organization gives rise to Full Relation, FlashRelation, Linear Relation, Hybrid Relation, and a KV-style Relation Cache. Across matched decoder-only models at approximately 10M, 30M, and 100M parameters, Full Relation achieves lower final validation NLL than MHA at all three scales. In a fixed-context reference benchmark, FlashRelation is 3.60-4.41x faster than the materialized Full Relation implementation. Across scale-matched production workloads, it reaches 76.4-84.9% of PyTorch FlashAttention throughput while executing the Full Relation operator. Hybrid Relation uses 75% Linear Relation layers and achieves strong language-modeling quality. These results support a relation-first view of token mixing: ask Self, ask Others, then let Flow follow Relation.

</details>


### [140] [A Standardized Framework for Machine Learning in Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20181)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro,Christian Bergler,Johann Jäger,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向标准化的评估框架，通过明确定义七个研究维度来提高机器学习电力系统保护方法评估的可比性和可审计性，并在PROTECT-90基准数据集上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习电力系统保护研究普遍报告近乎完美的性能分数，但这些分数的含义高度依赖于评估设置。保护任务、物理范围、测量、时序、目标、预处理和验证等要素往往共同变化且描述不完整，导致研究结果难以比较和复现。

Method: 提出标准化评估框架，定义七个必需的研究维度：保护目标、物理范围、可观测性、时序和决策窗口、目标与样本有效性、验证协议、评估输出。在PROTECT-90电磁瞬态基准数据集上实例化该框架，使用9022个仿真场景评估MLP模型在故障分类和定位任务上的表现。

Result: MLP模型在集中式传感、20ms决策窗口下，分类任务五折交叉验证宏平均F1分数达到0.991±0.001，定位平均绝对误差为线路长度10.20±0.25%。分类任务性能优于定位任务。降低可观测性使定位误差翻倍但对分类影响较小。传统双端定位器在更丰富的信息条件下优于学习定位器，且清洁预测性能不能决定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架将评估假设转化为显式、可复现的证据，为机器学习保护功能提供更可比、可审计的评估基础，并支持未来面向认证的评估。研究表明评估设计本身应作为科学贡献的一部分，以推进领域的标准化发展。

Abstract: Studies of machine-learning-based power-system protection increasingly report near-perfect scores, yet the meaning of those scores depends strongly on the evaluation setting. Protection task, physical scope, measurements, timing, targets, preprocessing, and validation often vary jointly and remain incompletely specified. This paper proposes a standardization-oriented framework that treats evaluation design as part of the scientific contribution. It defines seven required study dimensions: protection objective, physical scope, observability, timing and decision windows, targets and sample validity, validation protocol, and evaluation outputs. The framework is instantiated in a bounded case study on the public PROTECT-90 electromagnetic-transient benchmark, comprising 9022 simulated episodes from a 90 kV double-line topology, for onset-conditioned fault classification and localization. Under centralized sensing, simulation-metadata-aligned 20 ms windows, and episode-grouped validation, a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) achieved a five-fold mean macro-averaged F1 score of 0.991 +/- 0.001 for classification and a localization mean absolute error of 10.20 +/- 0.25% of line length (mean +/- std across episode-grouped folds). Extending the decision horizon to 50 ms preserved this task-dependent performance asymmetry, while reduced observability approximately doubled the MLP localization error but had little effect on classification. A synchronized two-ended conventional locator outperformed the learning locators under its richer clean information set, and measurement degradation showed that clean predictive performance did not determine robustness. The framework turns evaluation assumptions into explicit, reproducible evidence and provides a basis for more comparable, auditable evaluation and future certification-oriented assessment of machine-learning protection functions.

</details>


### [141] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个能够精确计算局部实对数典范阈值（RLCT）的确定性算法，解决了奇异模型信息准则中的关键计算难题，为深度学习等场景下的模型选择提供了新的理论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息准则（如BIC）依赖的正则性假设在奇异模型中失效，导致在深度学习等场景下模型选择错误。虽然WBIC使用局部学习系数λ来捕捉正确的边际似然渐近性，但其精确计算仅限于特殊情况，通常只能使用基于采样的估计方法。

Method: 提出首个确定性算法，能够对KL散度接触等价于多项式的任意二维模型精确计算局部RLCT。推导了算法复杂度界限，并将其应用于包括多项式神经网络在内的广泛模型类别。
