Result: 该算法不仅为校准基于采样的估计器提供了真实基准，还揭示了学习系数中采样方法无法发现的代数结构。在浅层模型情况下，精确计算速度优于采样方法。

Conclusion: 确定性计算方法为奇异模型的贝叶斯信息准则提供了精确可行的解决方案，在理论分析（揭示代数结构）和实际计算（浅层模型速度优势）两方面都具有采样方法不可替代的价值。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [142] [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究证明了通过非侵入式EEG可以从静默阅读中解码开放词汇的词级信息，解码性能随训练数据量呈对数线性增长且未饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内心语音解码面临根本性数据问题：无法收集与自发内心独白配对的脑活动数据，而现有的代理范式（提示重复性和回顾性报告的生成性内心语音）存在采集速度慢、时间锁定差、受试者依从性不可验证等问题。

Method: 采用静默阅读作为可扩展的代理任务，使用快速序列视觉呈现（RSVP）呈现连续叙事文本中的单词，每次试验随机化排版以部分解耦单词身份与低级视觉形式。训练卷积EEG编码器（可选后接因果Transformer），采用CLIP风格对比目标将短EEG窗口与来自大语言模型的单词隐藏状态嵌入对齐。数据来自单一高密度采样参与者的约24万次单词呈现，跨越393次运行（约49小时）的19通道干电极EEG。

Result: 解码评估为词分组的前10检索，性能可靠地高于机会水平，扩展到中频和罕见词汇，且随训练数据量呈对数线性增长，无饱和迹象。移除枕叶和后颞电极使词级增益减少约三分之一，但语境跟踪保持不变。对照分析将词级解码与叙事语境跟踪以及Transformer位置嵌入引入的非神经位置先验分离开来。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了开放词汇的词级信息可从静默阅读期间的EEG中恢复，解码受限于数据而非已饱和，为未来内心语音解码研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Non-invasive decoding of inner speech faces a fundamental data problem: a corpus pairing brain activity with a person's spontaneous inner monologue cannot be collected, and the available proxy paradigms (cued repetitive and retrospectively reported generative inner speech) are slow to acquire, poorly time-locked, and subject compliance is unverifiable. We therefore treat silent reading as a scalable proxy task and ask how much lexical and semantic information a contrastive decoder can extract from it. We report an open-vocabulary analysis of approximately 240,000 word presentations recorded from a single densely-sampled participant across 393 runs (ca. 49 h) of 19-channel dry-electrode EEG. Words from continuous narrative text were presented in rapid serial visual presentation, with typography randomised on every trial to partially decorrelate word identity from low-level visual form. A convolutional EEG encoder, optionally followed by a causal transformer, was trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective to align short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model. Decoding, evaluated as word-grouped top-10 retrieval against permutation baselines, was reliably above chance, extended to mid-frequency and rare words, and scaled log-linearly with training-data volume with no sign of saturation. Removing occipital and posterior-temporal electrodes reduced the word-level gain by roughly one third but left context tracking unchanged. Control analyses separate word-level decoding from narrative context tracking and from a non-neural positional prior introduced by the transformer's positional embedding. These results establish that open-vocabulary word-level information is recoverable from EEG during silent reading, and that decoding is data-limited rather than saturated.

</details>


### [143] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DICS框架，通过聚类方法构建紧凑的候选分裂点集合，在保持分类准确率的同时显著降低决策树训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树训练在大型高维数据集上计算开销大，主要瓶颈在于每个节点需要穷举搜索所有候选分裂点。

Method: 提出数据驱动的质心分裂(DICS)框架，利用聚类方法构建紧凑且信息丰富的候选分裂集合，通过融合类别感知结构来缩减分类任务的分裂搜索空间。

Result: 理论分析证明在给定假设下DICS不会降低分类树性能；实验表明在合成和基准数据集上，DICS在保持相当准确率的同时大幅减少训练时间。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动的先验知识融入分裂选择过程中，能够实现可扩展的分类树学习，对决策树、随机森林和梯度提升模型均适用。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [144] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用PCMCI+算法从家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试数据中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，揭示了不同性别和年龄亚组间因果结构的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动力学复杂且在不同患者群体中存在差异，这种复杂性阻碍了针对性干预措施的开发，因此需要深入理解不同亚组间的因果结构差异。

Method: 采用PCMCI+算法分析105例HSAT记录的窗口化分数变量，通过边黑名单方法融入领域知识，并使用Bootstrap聚合技术解决小样本亚组规模问题，构建动态因果图。

Result: 学习得到的因果图显示，时间自依赖性和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有队列中持续存在，而其他关系在不同亚组间存在显著差异，揭示了性别和年龄组间因果结构的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 该研究成功揭示了睡眠呼吸障碍在不同性别和年龄亚组中的因果结构差异，为开发针对性干预措施提供了重要的理论基础。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [145] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对字典学习后稀疏表示的物理支持推断方法，解决了高相关性字典中物理意义不确定的问题，并开发了自适应算法避免过度精确的物理报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高相关性字典中，字典学习后的稀疏追求可能产生精确的原子支持，但其物理解释可能与校准数据不一致。替代的校准兼容字典可能为相同的支持分配不同的物理意义，这导致了物理支持推断的不确定性问题。

Method: 开发了分辨率感知的物理支持推断方法，联合考虑学习字典和部署信号表示的不确定性。引入交叉字典置信度对应方法，保留校准兼容字典和部署兼容稀疏表示，然后将幸存的解释投影到物理支持空间。提出自适应有限银行程序——主动端点括号法(AEB)，仅评估仍能影响物理报告的候选者。

Result: 对于分离尺度为s的局部相关原子类，N个校准信号的最小最大物理分辨率满足δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s,1/(√N·s²)}，相对分辨率由方向信息尺度Ns⁶决定。部署复制仅在方向变化无法通过调整活动系数吸收时才改善物理定位。有限银行实验表明，点值插件选择器可能物理上过度精确，而AEB避免不支持细化且候选评估次数更少。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了高相关性字典中物理支持推断的不确定性问题，提供了理论分辨率界限，并通过AEB算法实现了计算效率与物理准确性的平衡。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [146] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BERT-LER模型，这是一个基于BERT架构的电子健康记录(EHR)预测模型，能够处理实验室检验数值并提供可解释性，在75M患者数据集上预训练，在EHRShot基准和哮喘严重程度研究中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结构化电子健康记录(EHRs)的预测模型很少同时强调定量实验室信息以及对输入医疗事件的可解释性，存在方法学上的空白。需要统一实验室数值表示和可解释性的框架。

Method: 提出了BERT-LER模型：(1)采用BERT风格架构处理编码化EHR时间线；(2)将实验室检验结果编码为离散tokens，通过基于百分位数的分箱保留梯度信息；(3)结合Integrated Gradients提供基于输入EHR序列的token级归因分析；(4)在75M患者的去标识化EHR数据集上预训练和微调。

Result: 在EHRShot基准套件和哮喘严重程度进展研究上进行评估：(1)预测性能与公开基准模型相当，在实验室相关任务上经常超越现有模型；(2)提供的归因结果与临床已知风险因素一致；(3)模型能够泛化到标准临床预测任务之外。

Conclusion: 成功填补了EHR基础建模中的方法学空白，在单一框架内统一了实验室数值表示和可解释性。该架构和可解释性方法可应用于多个治疗领域和预测任务，适用于基于结构化EHR训练的语言模型。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [147] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在相同部署条件下对比评估了天花板安装的FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi三种RF感知技术，发现IR-UWB在人体活动识别任务中表现最优，FMCW在未知环境泛化能力最佳，揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频感知研究中，不同技术（FMCW雷达、IR-UWB、Wi-Fi感知）缺乏在相同部署条件下的公平对比，且天花板安装雷达的性能研究不足，而其在医疗环境中具有实际部署和成本优势。

Method: 在六个房间布局中采集20名参与者的同步录制数据，使用相同的卷积神经网络(CNN)评估三种技术，测试细粒度10类人体活动识别(HAR)和粗粒度4类睡眠监测两项任务。

Result: IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中达到最高性能(89.0% macro F1)，FMCW在未知房间布局泛化方面表现最佳(83.8% macro F1)；睡眠监测任务中，所有技术在未知环境下均超过92% macro F1。

Conclusion: 研究发现识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间存在根本性权衡，这种差异可通过距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留等参数解释，为医疗RF感知系统设计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Online Scheduling for Throughput Maximization of Time-varying Markovian Channels with Unknown Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19398)
*Tasmeen Zaman Ornee,Clement Kam,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AoCSI的无线调度方法，在信道统计信息未知的条件下通过在线学习实现高效调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于信念状态(belief states)的无线调度方法需要已知的信道统计信息，但在实际场景中信道统计往往是未知的，导致信念状态计算不可行。此外，CSI获取成本高昂且只能通过ACK/NACK反馈在调度传输后获得，导致观测到的CSI可能已过时。

Method: 引入信道状态信息年龄(AoCSI)来表征CSI的陈旧程度，证明了最新观测的CSI及其AoCSI是历史信息的充分统计量，从而将无限信念状态空间简化为有限状态空间。在此基础上，将问题建模为不安分多臂赌博机(RMAB)，并提出在线最大增益优先(Online-MGF)策略。针对ON/OFF信道的特殊情况，证明了指数性(indexability)并推导了Whittle指数的闭式表达式。

Result: Online-MGF策略实现了关于回合数的次线性遗憾(sub-linear regret)；对于ON/OFF信道，证明了指数性并推导了Whittle指数的闭式表达式。数值实验表明，Online-MGF策略在极少的回合内就能收敛到已知统计信息下的MGF和Whittle指数策略。

Conclusion: 通过引入AoCSI作为充分统计量，成功解决了信道统计信息未知条件下的无线调度问题，为实际无线网络中资源调度提供了理论支撑和实用算法。

Abstract: We consider a wireless scheduling problem in downlink wireless networks with unknown channel statistics, where a Base Station (BS) sends data to multiple users. The scheduling performance relies heavily on accurate Channel State Information (CSI), which is often costly to acquire. In this paper, CSI is obtained from ACK/NACK feedback, only after each scheduled transmission. Due to limited wireless channel resources, all users cannot be scheduled for transmission simultaneously. Hence, the most recently observed CSI can be outdated. The traditional approach to solve scheduling problems using outdated CSI is to utilize belief states, which are calculated using the time correlation statistics of channels. However, channel statistics are often unknown; consequently, belief states can be uncountable and this approach becomes infeasible. In this paper, we introduce a new sufficient statistics for the wireless scheduling problem. Towards this effort, we characterize the CSI staleness by the Age of Channel State Information (AoCSI) and show that the latest observed CSI and its AoCSI is a sufficient statistic of the history to make the scheduling decisions. Accordingly, we are able to reduce the state space for online learning. Our goal is to develop an online scheduling algorithm that maximizes the expected sum throughput of all users over a finite time-horizon while satisfying a channel resource constraint. The formulated problem is a Restless Multi-armed Bandit (RMAB). We develop an online Maximum Gain First (Online-MGF) policy, which achieves sub-linear regret on the number of episodes. For a special case of ON/OFF channels, we are able to prove indexability and derive a closed-form expression of the Whittle index. Numerical results demonstrate that the Online-MGF policy converges to MGF and Whittle index policies with known statistics within a very few episodes.

</details>


### [149] [Empirical Evaluation of Cross-Carrier MCPTT & OTT MCX Interoperability in High-Density Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19554)
*Eman Hammad,Derek Ladd,Sridhar Kowdley,Walt Magnussen,Michael Fox*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究在德州农工大学Kyle Field球场（10.5万+观众）进行了多运营商MCPTT服务实地测试，发现网络拥塞导致的传输层抖动超过临界阈值会引起语音质量急剧下降，而优先级QoS通道可有效缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人群密集的大规模活动中，多机构应急响应使用共享商业基础设施部署宽带关键任务即按即讲（MCPTT）服务时，会面临资源竞争问题。现有研究缺乏在真实饱和约束下对跨运营商互操作性和不同QoS框架性能的实证评估。

Method: 设计了一项多运营商实地实验，在德州农工大学Kyle Field球场的一场10.5万+观众的橄榄球比赛期间，在多个物理扇区部署12部相同智能手机。使用感知客观语音质量分析（POLQA）、数据包传输指标和连接率来监控自动语音呼叫性能。

Result: 语音路径故障源于网络基础设施瓶颈而非设备硬件限制。研究发现了一种急剧的非线性网络故障模型：当传输层抖动超过临界阈值时，去抖动缓冲区欠载会导致结构性音频退化。优先级管理通道能够有效绕过这种拥塞。

Conclusion: 本研究为应急规划者提供了操作指导，建议强制要求网络基础设施支持端到端网络切片和专用资源调配，确保传输层抖动保持在临界故障边界以下，以保障MCPTT服务在关键时刻的可靠性。

Abstract: Deploying broadband Mission-Critical Push-To-Talk (MCPTT) services over shared commercial infrastructures introduces resource contention during multi-agency responses in mass-crowd events. This study evaluates cross-carrier interoperability and standard versus prioritized quality of service (QoS) frameworks under real-world saturation constraints. We design an empirical multi-carrier field experiment utilizing twelve identical smartphones deployed across multiple physical sectors inside Texas A&M University's Kyle Field during a football game with 105,000+ attendees. Automated voice calls were monitored using Perceptual Objective Listening Quality Analysis (POLQA), packet delivery metrics, and connection rates. The results reveal that voice path failure is isolated to network infrastructure bottlenecks rather than device hardware limitations. Specifically, we identify a sharp, non-linear network failure model where transport-layer jitter exceeding a critical threshold de-jitter buffer underflows, causing structural audio degradation. Priority-managed channels effectively bypass this congestion. This study helps establish an operational insight for emergency planners to mandate network infrastructure, end-to-end network slicing and dedicated resource provisioning capable of keeping transport-layer jitter below the critical failure boundary.

</details>


### [150] [Vorticity Dissipation Based Routing: A Fluid-Kinetic Framework for Loop-Free Transport in Ultra-Dense Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19630)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Weiwei Jiang,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于涡度耗散的路由(VDR)框架，通过Helmholtz-Hodge分解将宏观流量正交解耦为无旋需求分量和有环涡度分量，实现了超密集无线网络中路由环路的宏观量化与抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超密集无线网络中的离散路由协议受限于信令开销和瞬时路由环路导致的频谱效率下降问题。现有连续介质标量密度建模方法缺乏表征拓扑异常所需的矢量几何结构，无法有效描述路由环路等拓扑问题。

Method: 利用Helmholtz-Hodge分解建立流体动力学框架，将宏观流量正交解耦为无旋分量和有环分量；定义网络涡度作为量化拓扑低效性的宏观指标；将路由优化表述为盘旋度泛函上的梯度流，推导涡度耗散方程作为支配动力学定律。

Result: Lyapunov稳定性分析证明了全局盘旋度单调衰减至渐近无环平衡态；数值实验验证VDR能够抑制实际转发环路、降低端到端时延、维持稳健的分组投递率；在固定面积密集化场景下呈现近线性扩展性，并显式考虑了网格相关的泊松求解器开销。

Conclusion: 所提出的VDR框架成功将流体动力学理论应用于无线网络路由优化，通过矢量几何建模实现了路由环路的宏观表征与抑制，为超密集网络的可扩展高效路由提供了理论保证和实践方案。

Abstract: Discrete routing protocols in ultra-dense wireless networks are constrained by signaling overhead and transient routing loops that degrade radio-resource efficiency. While continuum modeling provides a scalable alternative, existing scalar density approaches lack the vector geometric structure required to characterize these topological anomalies. This paper introduces a fluid-kinetic framework, vorticity dissipation-based routing (VDR), utilizing the Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition. We demonstrate that the macroscopic traffic flux can be orthogonally decoupled into a demand-driven irrotational component and a loop-induced solenoidal component representing routing vorticity. Building on this insight, we define network vorticity as a macroscopic metric to quantify topological inefficiency. Routing optimization is formulated as a gradient flow on an enstrophy functional, yielding a vorticity dissipation equation as the governing dynamic law. Lyapunov stability analysis proves that this mechanism ensures the monotonic decay of global enstrophy toward an asymptotically loop-free equilibrium. Numerical results validate that VDR suppresses realized forwarding loops, reduces end-to-end delay, maintains robust packet delivery, and exhibits near-linear scaling under fixed-area densification while explicitly accounting for the grid-dependent Poisson-solver cost.

</details>


### [151] [Digital Tides: A Fluid-Dynamic Framework for Flux-Aware Infrastructure Provisioning in UAV Logistics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19638)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Song Zhao,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对高频脉冲式物流无人机群形成的"数字潮汐"交通动态，提出基于流体力学的通量感知非对称激活策略，通过求解连续性方程预测计算负载传播方向，实现零延迟追踪移动波前，在服务可靠性和能耗之间达到帕累托最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基础设施资源配置策略依赖静态快照分析和局部密度估计，无法捕捉计算负载的宏观平流运动，导致反应式资源激活存在滞后性，在前进波前处以牺牲关键任务服务为代价换取名义效率增益。

Method: 建立基于流体力学的时空框架，通过显式求解连续性方程刻画负载流的宏观速度场；提出通量感知非对称激活策略，利用导出的信息通量向量作为需求传播的运动学前兆，解耦激活与去活动态，生成前瞻性保护环以补偿服务启动延迟。

Result: 理论分析证实通量信号具有内在的空间相位超前特性；推导了瞬时服务可用率和周期平均能效的闭式表达式；数值结果表明所提策略实现零延迟追踪移动波前，在动态物流走廊中服务可靠性和能耗方面优于反应式基准方案。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了高频脉冲式无人机集群场景下的可持续资源配置问题，通量驱动策略通过主动预测需求传播方向，有效补偿边缘计算容器冷启动延迟，为移动计算网络中复杂交通动态的资源调配提供了新的理论框架和实践方案。

Abstract: The emergence of high-frequency pulsating logistics unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms gives rise to ``Digital Tides'', i.e., complex traffic dynamics that challenge sustainable resource provisioning in mobile computing networks. Conventional infrastructure provisioning strategies, which typically rely on static snapshot-based analysis and localized density estimation, fail to capture the macroscopic advection of computational workloads. As a result, reactive resource activation suffers from inherent hysteresis, yielding nominal efficiency gains at the cost of mission-critical service loss at the advancing wavefront. To address this issue, we develop a fluid-based spatiotemporal framework by explicitly solving the continuity equation to characterize the macroscopic velocity field of the workload flow. Building on this framework, we propose a flux-aware asymmetric activation strategy that leverages the derived information flux vector as a kinematic precursor of demand propagation. Unlike symmetric thresholding, the proposed control logic decouples activation and deactivation dynamics. Theoretical analysis confirms the intrinsic spatial phase-lead of the flux signal and shows that the proposed strategy generates a proactive guard ring to compensate for service setup latency, including delays caused by mobile edge computing container cold-starts. We further derive closed-form expressions for instantaneous service availability and period-average energy efficiency. In addition, we formulate a quality-of-service-penalized metric to evaluate effective energy efficiency under strict outage constraints. Numerical results show that the proposed flux-driven strategy enables zero-latency tracking of the mobile wavefront and achieves a Pareto-optimal trade-off between service reliability and energy consumption, outperforming reactive baselines in dynamic logistics corridors.

</details>


### [152] [RFWM: Physics-Guided World Model for Dynamic Wireless Radiance Field Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19709)
*Zijiu Yang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出RFWM，一种物理引导的射频世界模型，通过两阶段训练策略和多物理约束，实现了从多模态物理条件到时空射频场的映射，在动态和未见环境中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频辐射场建模对无线网络优化和感知至关重要，但在动态和未见环境中仍具挑战性。现有基于学习的方法虽然能从稀疏测量中合成射频场，但大多数难以泛化到动态和未见环境。

Method: RFWM采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段将预训练的视觉扩散主干适配到射频轨迹，并使用Friis引导先验进行粗衰减指导；第二阶段从头训练ControlNet并微调射频适配主干，通过六个物理引导正则化器强制执行细粒度传播一致性，跨高度头联合生成不同接收器高度的射频轨迹。

Result: 构建了包含7,715个序列的新基准数据集，平均33帧，覆盖115个环境。实验结果表明，RFWM在分布内和分布外设置下分别比最先进方法提高约7 dB和3 dB的MSE性能。

Conclusion: RFWM成功地将多模态物理条件映射到时空射频场，通过物理引导的先验和约束实现了在动态和未见环境中的有效泛化，为射频辐射场建模提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) radiance-field modeling is essential for wireless network optimization and sensing, yet remains challenging in dynamic and unseen environments. Existing learning-based methods synthesize RF fields from sparse measurements, but most struggle to generalize to dynamic and unseen environments. To address this limitation, we propose RFWM, a physics-guided RF world model that maps multimodal physical conditions like visual dynamics and AP configurations to spatiotemporal RF fields. RFWM adopts a two-stage training strategy with physics-guided priors and constraints. In the first stage, RFWM adapts a pretrained visual diffusion backbone to RF trajectories to predict RF sequences from a few past RF inputs, while conditioning the backbone on a Friis-guided prior for coarse attenuation guidance. In the second stage, RFWM learns the physical-to-RF mapping by training a ControlNet from scratch and fine-tuning the RF-adapted backbone, while six physics-guided regularizers enforce fine-grained propagation consistency. Cross-height heads then jointly generate RF trajectories at queried receiver heights in one forward pass. We construct a new benchmark of 7,715 sequences averaging 33 frames across 115 environments for dynamic RF-field generation. Experimental results show that RFWM improves MSE by approximately 7 dB and 3 dB over the state of the art under in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings, respectively.

</details>


### [153] [Proper Sea Surface Roughness Enhances the Performance of Near-Shore Maritime Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19730)
*Wen-Yu Dong,Shaoshi Yang,Song Zhao,Jinyang Yu,Weiliang Xie,Rui-Si Han,Qi Bi,Sheng Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于随机几何的近岸海事无线通信分析框架，通过引入与海浪高度相关的反射系数，首次实现了粗糙海面条件下的覆盖概率和平均速率的解析表达，揭示了海况对可靠性与容量权衡的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析模型依赖过于简化的传播假设（如完全平滑的海面），无法充分捕捉海事信道的动态特性，缺乏对真实近岸环境的准确性能分析。

Method: 采用非齐次泊松点过程模拟船舶空间分布，基于瑞利判据推导了与有效波高显式关联的新型反射系数，将粗糙度感知的信道模型整合到随机几何框架中，推导上行链路覆盖概率和平均遍历速率的解析表达式。

Result: 首次实现了动态条件下的聚合干扰的可处理表征；分析揭示了海况依赖的可靠性-容量权衡关系：粗糙度引起的相干镜面反射衰减可抑制破坏性干扰零点并提高面向可靠性的覆盖，但会降低高信干噪比和平均速率性能；跨频率消融实验证实了粗糙效应的波长依赖性。

Conclusion: 粗糙海面条件下的结果作为模型预测呈现，需要直接VHF验证；该框架为近岸海事通信系统的设计与优化提供了物理基础的性能分析工具，强调了在实际系统中评估特定工作频率下反射系数的重要性。

Abstract: Accurate performance analysis for near-shore maritime wireless communication is essential for ensuring robust and reliable operations. However, existing analytical models often rely on oversimplified propagation assumptions, such as a perfectly smooth sea surface, which fail to capture the full dynamics of the maritime channel. In this paper, we develop a physically grounded analytical framework using stochastic geometry that bridges this gap. The spatial distribution of vessels is modeled as a non-homogeneous Poisson point process to reflect realistic near-port densities. We replace the idealized smooth-sea assumption by deriving a novel reflection coefficient from the classical Rayleigh criterion, which explicitly links the path loss to the significant wave height. Integrating this roughness-aware channel model into the stochastic geometry framework, we derive new analytical expressions for the uplink coverage probability and average ergodic rate, providing the first tractable characterization of aggregate interference under such dynamic conditions. The analysis reveals a sea-state-dependent reliability--capacity trade-off: roughness-induced attenuation of the coherent specular reflection can suppress destructive-interference nulls and improve reliability-oriented coverage, while reducing high-SINR and average-rate performance. Available measurements support the underlying roughness-sensitive reflection mechanism, but direct VHF validation under rough sea conditions remains unavailable; the corresponding rough-sea results are therefore interpreted as model-based predictions. A cross-frequency ablation further confirms the wavelength dependence of the roughness effect and shows that the reflection coefficient must be evaluated for the operating frequency.

</details>


### [154] [Using Zone-Disjoint Multi-Path Routing Algorithm for Video Transmission over Ah-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20148)
*Ali Asghar Khavasi,Nastooh Taheri Javan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对ad hoc网络中多路径路由的非完全独立问题，提出了一种新型区域不相交多路径路由算法，配合适当的视频传输方案，显著提升了视频传输性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ad hoc网络中的多路径路由由于CSMA/CA机制的影响，即使采用节点不相交路径，端到端延迟和误码率仍会因邻居节点的通信模式而相互依赖，导致中间节点被迫静默、延迟增加，影响多路径视频传输效果。

Method: 提出一种新型多路径路由算法，选择区域不相交（zone-disjoint）路径替代传统的节点不相交（node-disjoint）路径，并结合适配的视频传输方案进行视频多路径传输。

Result: 实验表明，新算法配合适当的视频传输方案，在端到端延迟和误码率等性能指标上优于传统算法，有效解决了路径间依赖问题。

Conclusion: 采用区域不相交路径的多路径路由算法能显著改善ad hoc网络中视频传输性能，为多路径路由设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Finding multi-path routes in ad hoc networks due to their grid topology seems to be a trivial task, but because of CSMA/CA effects in these networks found paths are not completely disjoint unless an appropriate algorithm have taken into account. If such an algorithm provided and designed carefully it could improve multi-path video transmission over these kinds of networks. By using node-disjoint paths, it is expected that the end-to-end delay and BER in each case should be independent of each other. However, because of natural properties and medium access mechanisms in ad hoc networks the end-to-end delay and also BER between any source and destination depends on the pattern of communication in the neighborhood region. In this case some of the intermediate nodes should be silent to reverence their neighbors and this matter increases the end-to-end delay. To avoid this problem, multi-path routing algorithms can use zone-disjoint paths instead of node-disjoint paths. In this paper we demonstrated a new multi-path routing algorithm that selects zone-disjoint paths that in addition used for video multi-path transmission over ad hoc networks. It is shown that by using this new algorithm along with choosing an appropriate scheme for video transmission over discovered paths, our approach receives enhanced results comparing previously used algorithms.

</details>
